===== FILE readers/probe/fake_pilot.py | 1666 bytes | sha256 ced22962ed9e492e1640458cc16d2172562ba45d37f2a7ab3ff4713316657e3c =====
import json, random, hashlib
random.seed(1)
calls = [json.loads(l) for l in open("calls_pilot.jsonl", encoding="utf-8")]
words_easy = "le chat la maison un chien joue dans le jardin avec sa sœur et son frère il mange une pomme rouge elle regarde le ciel bleu".split()
words_hard = "la croûte terrestre le magma s'échappe lentement des scientifiques surveillent ces phénomènes géologiques grâce à des instruments précis".split()
out = open("data/raw/calls_pilot_fake.jsonl", "w", encoding="utf-8")
for c in calls:
    if c["kind"] == "text":
        age = {"3H":6.5,"4H":7.5,"5H":8.5,"6H":9.5,"7H":10.5,"8H":11.5}[c["grade"]]
        ph = (age-6.5)/5
        n = int(random.gauss(60 + 25*age, 20))
        toks = [random.choice(words_hard) if random.random() < ph*0.6 else random.choice(words_easy) for _ in range(max(n, 5))]
        text = "Titre du texte\n\n" + " ".join(toks).capitalize() + "."
        if c["label"] == "L1" and c["grade"] == "3H" and random.random() < 0.5:
            text = "Je ne peux pas."  # short -> excluded
        finish = "stop"
    else:
        text = f"Les élèves de {c['grade']} ont entre {c['truth_age_from']} et {c['truth_age_to']} ans."
        finish = "stop"
    rec = dict(c, provider="x", model_requested="x", model_returned="x-2026", sampling={"temperature": 1.0}, status=200, ok=True,
               finish_reason=finish, text=text, input_tokens=50, output_tokens=len(text)//4, cost_usd=0.0, latency_s=1.0,
               utc_start="t", utc="t", text_sha256=hashlib.sha256(text.encode()).hexdigest(), response={})
    out.write(json.dumps(rec, ensure_ascii=False) + "\n")
out.close()
print("wrote", len(calls))

import sys, math
sys.path.insert(0, "/tmp/claude-0/-home-claude/d28672eb-31f4-533e-85fc-00a96d822022/scratchpad/verify")
import compute as C
LEX="/usr/local/lib/python3.13/dist-packages/pylexique/Lexique383/Lexique383.txt"
lex, sha = C.load_lexicon(LEX)
print("lexicon sha", sha[:12], "forms", len(lex))

def show(text, label):
    title, lines = C.clean(text)
    sents, rows = C.analyse(lines, lex)
    m, lemmas = C.measure(lines, lex)
    names = [t for t,_,nm in rows if nm]
    print(f"\n=== {label} ===  title={title!r}")
    print("names:", names)
    print("content lemmas:", lemmas)
    oov = [low for t,low,nm in rows if not nm and low not in C.ELIDED and low not in lex]
    print("OOV non-name:", oov)
    print({k: (round(v,3) if isinstance(v,float) else v) for k,v in m.items()})
    return m, lemmas, rows

# 1. dialogue with colon + guillemets, names at sentence start, passé simple, elisions, hyphens
t1 = """**Le secret de la forêt**

Léa marcha longtemps dans la forêt. Soudain, elle s’arrêta : « Regarde, Hugo ! Un renard ! »
Hugo s’approcha doucement. « N’aie pas peur », murmura-t-il. Le renard les regarda, puis s’enfuit.
« Où va-t-il ? » demanda Léa. Hugo haussa les épaules : « Peut-être chez lui, là-bas, près du vieux chêne. »
Aujourd’hui encore, quelqu’un raconte cette histoire au village. D’abord, personne n’y croyait. C’est-à-dire que les grands-parents riaient.
Maman dit : « Viens manger ! » Papa, lui, lisait le journal dans la salle à manger.
Qu’est-ce que tu fais ? Allons-y ! Dis-le-moi ! Les enfants s’écrièrent : « Hourra ! »"""
m1, l1, r1 = show(t1, "dialogue/passé simple")

# 2. determiners: ADJ:pos / ADJ:dem / ADJ:ind / ADJ:num
t2 = "Mon chat et ma sœur jouent dans ce jardin avec ces fleurs. Chaque matin, tout le monde mange deux pommes et quelques fraises. Son frère a plusieurs amis."
show(t2, "determiners")
for w in ["mon","ma","ses","ce","cette","ces","chaque","tout","tous","deux","trois","quelques","plusieurs","autre","même","quel","aucun","certains","tel","leur","notre","premier"]:
    e = lex.get(w)
    print(w, e[0] if e else None, e[1] if e else None, round(C.zipf(e[2]),2) if e else None)

===== FILE readers/probe/p3.py | 3393 bytes | sha256 50fa635da8ba16b9dfa9ba75305c6e4473b4a4004d754301e3bb2498bbeff692 =====
import sys, re
sys.path.insert(0, "/tmp/claude-0/-home-claude/d28672eb-31f4-533e-85fc-00a96d822022/scratchpad/verify")
import pandas as pd, compute as C
LEX="/usr/local/lib/python3.13/dist-packages/pylexique/Lexique383/Lexique383.txt"
df = pd.read_csv(LEX, sep="\t", dtype=str, encoding="latin-1")
df.columns = [re.sub(r"^\d+_", "", c) for c in df.columns]
print(sorted(df["cgram"].fillna("").unique()))
for c in ["freqfilms2","freqlivres"]:
    df[c] = df[c].fillna("0").str.replace(",", ".").astype(float)
sub = df[df["cgram"].fillna("").str.startswith("ADJ:")]
print("ADJ:* rows", len(sub), "distinct forms", sub["ortho"].nunique())
print("film freq mass per million of ADJ:* forms:", round(sub["freqfilms2"].sum()), " vs all NOM:", round(df[df.cgram=="NOM"]["freqfilms2"].sum()), " all plain ADJ:", round(df[df.cgram=="ADJ"]["freqfilms2"].sum()))
# forms with oe where LLM would write œ
oe = df[df["ortho"].str.contains("oe", na=False) & (df.cgram.isin(["NOM","ADJ","VER"]))].sort_values("freqfilms2", ascending=False)
print(oe[["ortho","cgram","freqfilms2"]].head(15).to_string())

lex,_ = C.load_lexicon(LEX)
def alt_measure(lines, drop_adj_sub=True, fold_oe=True):
    # recompute the mean with (a) exact-cgram ADJ only, (b) oe folding
    sents, rows = C.analyse(lines, lex)
    vals = []
    for t, low, name in rows:
        if name or low in C.ELIDED: continue
        key = low.replace("œ","oe").replace("æ","ae") if fold_oe else low
        e = lex.get(key)
        if e is None:
            vals.append(C.zipf(0)); continue
        cat, lemma, lf, _, _ = e
        if cat in C.CONTENT and lemma not in C.EXCLUDED_LEMMAS:
            if drop_adj_sub and full_cat.get(key, "").startswith("ADJ:"): continue
            vals.append(C.zipf(lf))
    return sum(vals)/len(vals), len(vals)
# full cgram of the most frequent reading
df["formfreq"] = df.freqfilms2 + df.freqlivres
best = df.sort_values("formfreq", ascending=False).groupby("ortho").first()
full_cat = best["cgram"].fillna("").to_dict()

texts = {
"3H-like story": """Le petit lapin
Tom est un petit lapin. Il a une sœur. Sa sœur s'appelle Lili.
Tom et Lili jouent dans le jardin. Ils voient une fleur. La fleur est rouge.
Maman lapin dit : « Venez manger ! » Tom court. Lili court aussi.
Le soir, Tom a mal au cœur. Il a trop mangé de carottes ! Sa maman lui fait un câlin. Tom dort bien.""",
"8H-like info": """Les volcans
Un volcan est une ouverture de la croûte terrestre par laquelle s'échappent des roches en fusion, le magma. Lorsque le magma atteint la surface, on parle de lave. Certains volcans, comme ceux d'Islande, entrent en éruption régulièrement, tandis que d'autres dorment pendant plusieurs siècles.
Les scientifiques, appelés volcanologues, surveillent ces géants grâce à des sismographes. Chaque secousse, chaque dégagement de gaz, constitue un indice précieux. Au cœur de la Terre, la chaleur est telle que les roches fondent. Cette énergie façonne nos paysages depuis des millions d'années.""",
}
for k, t in texts.items():
    lines = C.clean(t)[1]
    m,_ = C.measure(lines, lex)
    a, n = alt_measure(lines, True, True)
    b, _ = alt_measure(lines, False, True)
    c, _ = alt_measure(lines, True, False)
    print(f"\n{k}: v0.2 mean={m['mean_zipf_content']:.3f} (n={m['n_content']}) | exact-ADJ+oe-fold={a:.3f} (n={n}) | oe-fold only={b:.3f} | exact-ADJ only={c:.3f}")

===== FILE readers/referee/clust_sim.py | 1257 bytes | sha256 8cf25dd45fbbf4fe3cd0f94491dd1fed7dbc6d59c39f326e2f44a45fbf57ffe0 =====
"""Referee check: type I error of the text-level OLS when the k replicates of a prompt share
an unmodelled prompt-level component (e.g. topic x model or grade x genre interactions)."""
import sys, numpy as np
sys.path.insert(0, "/tmp/claude-0/-home-claude/d28672eb-31f4-533e-85fc-00a96d822022/scratchpad/art5/sim")
import sim_power as S
from scipy import stats
rng = np.random.default_rng(7)
def run(k, sigma, cell_sd, reps=400):
    rows, X, names = S.build_design(6, k)
    c2, _ = S.contrasts(names, 6)
    XtX_inv = np.linalg.pinv(X.T @ X); P = XtX_inv @ X.T
    dof = X.shape[0] - np.linalg.matrix_rank(X); v2 = c2 @ XtX_inv @ c2
    # cell = model x grade x label x genre x topic (the prompt)
    cell = np.unique(rows, axis=0, return_inverse=True)[1].ravel()
    rej = 0
    for _ in range(reps):
        y = S.simulate_y(rows, 6, sigma, -0.04, 0.0)
        y = y + rng.normal(0, cell_sd, cell.max()+1)[cell]
        beta = P @ y; r = y - X @ beta; s2 = r @ r / dof
        t2 = (c2 @ beta) / np.sqrt(s2 * v2)
        rej += stats.t.cdf(t2, dof) <= 0.05
    return rej / reps
for k in (2, 4):
    for cell_sd in (0.0, 0.1, 0.2):
        print(f"k={k} sigma=0.20 prompt-level sd={cell_sd}: H2 rejection rate under null = {run(k, 0.20, cell_sd):.3f}")

import sys, math

def show(text):
    print("TEXT:", text)
    for s in M.tokenize(text):
        for w, proper in s:
            if w in lex.index:
                print(f"  {w:20s} proper={proper} cgram={lex.at[w,'cgram']:6s} freq={lex.at[w,'freq']:.3f} zipf={M.zipf(lex.at[w,'freq']):.2f} nbsyll={lex.at[w,'nbsyll']}")
            else:
                print(f"  {w:20s} proper={proper} OUT OF LEXICON -> zipf 1.0 counted as content")

===== FILE readers/referee/diag3.py | 1666 bytes | sha256 cb7e57fcdc01c1914c038827634fe4586e48d0e0c6e8812469e70f04197803f9 =====
import sys, math
sys.path.insert(0, "/tmp/claude-0/-home-claude/d28672eb-31f4-533e-85fc-00a96d822022/scratchpad/art5/sim")
import measures as M
lex = M.lexicon()
for w in ["j","n","s","c","m","t","jusqu","lorsqu","puisqu","quelqu","presqu","entr"]:
    print(w, "->", (lex.at[w,'cgram'], round(M.zipf(lex.at[w,'freq']),2)) if w in lex.index else "OOV")
# A realistic LLM-style story with typographic apostrophes, dialogue, inversion, title line
story_curly = """Le secret de la forêt

Léa adore se promener dans la forêt. Aujourd’hui, elle part avec son papa. Ils marchent sur le petit chemin.
— Regarde ! dit Léa. Un écureuil !
L’animal grimpe vite dans l’arbre. Léa rit.
« Il n’a pas peur de nous », dit-elle. Son papa sourit. « C’est parce que nous sommes calmes », explique-t-il.
Quelqu’un a laissé des noisettes près du tronc. Léa en prend une. L’écureuil redescend doucement. Léa est très contente."""
story_straight = story_curly.replace("’", "'")
def dump(text):
    for s in M.tokenize(text):
        print("  SENT:", [(w, "P" if p else "") for w, p in s])
    content = []
    for s in M.tokenize(text):
        for w, proper in s:
            if proper: continue
            if w in lex.index:
                if lex.at[w,"cgram"] in M.CONTENT_CGRAMS:
                    content.append((w, round(M.zipf(lex.at[w,"freq"]),2)))
            else:
                content.append((w+"[OOV]", 1.0))
    print("  CONTENT:", content)
    print("  ", {k: round(v,3) for k,v in M.measures(text).items()})
print("=== curly apostrophes (typical LLM output)")
dump(story_curly)
print("=== straight apostrophes")
dump(story_straight)

df = df.sort_values("formfreq", ascending=False)
best = df.groupby("ortho").first()
sub = {o: r["cgram"] for o, r in best.iterrows()}
lex2 = {}
n_changed = 0
for o, e in lex.items():
    cg = sub[o]
    if e[0] == "ADJ" and cg != "ADJ":
        lex2[o] = ("ADJX",) + e[1:]
        n_changed += 1
    else:
        lex2[o] = e
print("forms whose best reading is ADJ:<subtype>:", n_changed)

rows = []
for f in sorted(glob.glob("texts/*.txt")):
    txt = open(f, encoding="utf-8").read()
    _, lines = compute.clean(txt)
    m1, l1 = compute.measure(lines, lex)
    m2, l2 = compute.measure(lines, lex2)
    # which tokens are the extra "content" words
    _, rws = compute.analyse(lines, lex)
    extra = [t.lower() for t, low, nm in rws if not nm and low in lex and lex[low][0]=="ADJ" and sub[low] != "ADJ"]
    rows.append((os.path.basename(f), m1["n_content"], m2["n_content"], m1["mean_zipf_content"], m2["mean_zipf_content"],
                 m1["mean_zipf_content"]-m2["mean_zipf_content"], len(extra), sorted(set(extra))))
for r in rows:
    print(f"{r[0]:9s} n_content {r[1]:3d} -> {r[2]:3d} | mean {r[3]:.3f} -> {r[4]:.3f} | delta(deployed - fixed) {r[5]:+.3f} | extra tokens {r[6]} {r[7]}")

import sys, re

txt = """Une journée à la ferme

Ce matin-là, Léa se leva très tôt. Elle rejoignit son grand-père dans la cour. Il lui dit : « Viens, nous allons donner à manger aux poules. »
Léa prit le seau de grains et lança : « Regarde, elles courent toutes vers moi ! » Le grand-père rit de bon cœur.
Ensuite, ils allèrent voir les vaches. Léa murmura : « Écoute, elles mâchent l'herbe. » Son grand-père répondit : « Attention, ne les effraie pas. »

def go(t):
    _, lines = compute.clean(t)
    _, rows = compute.analyse(lines, lex)
    m, lem = compute.measure(lines, lex)
    return [x[0] for x in rows if x[2]], m["n_content"], m["mean_zipf_content"]

===== FILE readers/refute_work/n6_mc.py | 1598 bytes | sha256 ec69031af639debe0e98655a2fdf5b57fd6dbaa2acd1bd33e45605b5f6e5f63d =====
import sys
sys.path.insert(0, "/tmp/claude-0/-home-claude/d28672eb-31f4-533e-85fc-00a96d822022/scratchpad/refute_work/a")
import numpy as np, pandas as pd, warnings
import statsmodels.formula.api as smf
import analyze as A
warnings.filterwarnings("ignore")

def one(rng, sigma, slope, heavy=True):
    c = A.simulate_cubes({}, rng, n_models=3, n_topics=2, sigma=sigma, slope=slope, labels=["L5"], heavy=heavy)["L5"]  # (M,G,E,T)
    M, G, E, T = c.shape
    rows = [dict(model=f"m{m}", grade=A.GRADES[g], genre=f"e{e}", topic=f"t{t}", y=c[m, g, e, t])
            for m in range(M) for g in range(G) for e in range(E) for t in range(T)]
    d = pd.DataFrame(rows); d["age"] = d["grade"].map(A.AGE_MID)
    fit = smf.ols("y ~ age + C(model) + C(genre) + C(topic)", data=d).fit()
    return float(fit.params["age"]), float(fit.bse["age"]), len(d), fit.df_resid

rng = np.random.default_rng(12345)
print("n texts / df_resid:", one(rng, 0.3, -0.04)[2:])
R = 4000
for sigma in (0.15, 0.2, 0.3, 0.4):
    for true in (-0.02, -0.04, -0.06):
        sl, se = [], []
        for _ in range(R):
            s, e, *_ = one(rng, sigma, true)
            sl.append(s); se.append(e)
        sl = np.array(sl)
        print(f"sigma {sigma:.2f} true slope {true:+.2f}: mean SE {np.mean(se):.4f} | sd of slope_hat {sl.std():.4f} | P(slope_hat > -0.02, 'flat ruler stop') = {np.mean(sl > -0.02):.3f}")
print("analytic SE at sigma .3: ", 0.3/np.sqrt(12*2*(2.5**2+1.5**2+0.5**2)))
from scipy import stats
print("analytic false stop at true -0.04, sigma .3, df 66:", 1-stats.t.cdf((-0.02+0.04)/(0.3/np.sqrt(210)), 66))

    print(f"{n}: straight={straight:.3f} (n={ns})  typographic={curly:.3f} (n={nc})  typographic+normalised={fixed:.3f}  delta(curly-straight)={curly-straight:+.3f}")

    base, nb = S.mean_zipf(t)
    wo, nw = S.mean_zipf(t, stop={"ne", "pas"})

    toks = S.tokens(t)
    init = []
    # list names that sit at sentence start in measures.py's logic (i.e. not flagged proper)

        if w in [x.lower() for x in names[n]] and not p:
            init.append(w + ("(OOV z=1)" if w not in S.LEX.index else "(in lexicon z=%.2f)" % S.z(S.LEX.at[w, 'ff'])))
    print(f"{n}: as written={base:.3f} (n={nb})  names excluded={ex:.3f} (n={ne_})  swing={ex-base:+.3f}  unflagged names: {init}")

def content_tokens(t, **kw):
    out=[]
    for w,p in S.tokens(t, **kw):
        if p: continue
        if w in S.LEX.index:
            if S.LEX.at[w,"cgram"] in S.CONTENT: out.append(w)
        else: out.append(w)
    return out

print("=== A4: share of content tokens that are est/était/très/plus/bien (+ ne/pas) ===")
for n,t in texts.items():
    ct = content_tokens(t, fix_hyph=True, fix_aujourdhui=True, names=["Léa","Chloé","Pierre","Pilou","Louis","Emma","Camille","Etna","Piton"])
    k5 = sum(w in five for w in ct); k7 = sum(w in five|{"ne","pas"} for w in ct)
    base,_ = S.mean_zipf(t, fix_hyph=True, fix_aujourdhui=True, names=["Léa","Chloé","Pierre","Pilou","Louis","Emma","Camille","Etna","Piton"])
    w5,_ = S.mean_zipf(t, stop=five, fix_hyph=True, fix_aujourdhui=True, names=["Léa","Chloé","Pierre","Pilou","Louis","Emma","Camille","Etna","Piton"])
    w7,_ = S.mean_zipf(t, stop=five|{"ne","pas"}, fix_hyph=True, fix_aujourdhui=True, names=["Léa","Chloé","Pierre","Pilou","Louis","Emma","Camille","Etna","Piton"])
    print(f"{n}: content tokens={len(ct)}; five words={k5} ({100*k5/len(ct):.1f}%); +ne/pas={k7} ({100*k7/len(ct):.1f}%); mean {base:.3f} -> w/o five {w5:.3f} ({w5-base:+.3f}) -> w/o five+ne/pas {w7:.3f} ({w7-base:+.3f})")

print("mean Zipf of the seven forms: %.2f; typical rest of content words ~ 4.6 => each extra 1 percentage point of share moves the mean by ~%.3f Zipf" % (statistics.mean(zs), 0.01*(statistics.mean(zs)-4.6)))
print("reference: assumed slope -0.04 Zipf per year of age => one year of age = 0.04 Zipf")

    t = getattr(T, name)
    ref = M.measures(t)
    mine, n = S.mean_zipf(t)
    flag = abs(ref["mean_zipf_content"] - mine) < 1e-12
    ok &= flag
    print(f"{name:7s} measures.py={ref['mean_zipf_content']:.6f} mine(all fixes off)={mine:.6f} n_content={n} match={flag}")

    print(f"  prompt SD {sc:.2f} ({sc/0.2:.2f} x within SD, ICC {icc:.2f}): HC3 {r['hc3']:.3f} | cluster-robust by cell {r['cr_cell']:.3f} | unadjusted one-sided p<=.05 {r['p2_unadj_hc3']:.3f}")

===== FILE readers/tmp_refute/d15_sim.py | 6383 bytes | sha256 214890f362a021a65fb83bf30c9f705e5a636bdf1d4fcf73d3ff386c31e0b67a =====
"""D15: null rejection rate of H2 under text-level HC3 when texts from the same prompt cell share a random effect.
Re-uses build_design / contrasts from sim_power.py. Only noise is simulated: under the null every systematic
component (model x grade, topic, genre) lies in the column space of X, so it cannot change the test."""
import sys, time
import numpy as np
from scipy import stats, sparse
sys.path.insert(0, "/tmp/refute")
import loadmods as L
SP = L.simpower()
ALPHA = 0.05

def setup(n_models, k):
    rows, X, names = SP.build_design(n_models, k)
    c2, c3 = SP.contrasts(names, n_models)
    XtX_inv = np.linalg.pinv(X.T @ X)
    P = XtX_inv @ X.T
    h = np.einsum("ij,jk,ik->i", X, XtX_inv, X)
    dof = X.shape[0] - np.linalg.matrix_rank(X)
    n = X.shape[0]
    cell = np.arange(n) // k                      # (model, grade, label, genre, topic) cell; replicate index is the last product factor
    m, g, c, ge, t = rows.T
    pid = (((g - 3) * 5 + c) * 2 + ge) * SP.TOPICS + t   # prompt string (grade,label,genre,topic), shared by the 6 models
    return dict(rows=rows, X=X, names=names, c2=c2, c3=c3, XtX_inv=XtX_inv, P=P, h=h, dof=dof, n=n, k=k,
                cell=cell, pid=pid, a2=P.T @ c2, a3=P.T @ c3, v2=c2 @ XtX_inv @ c2, v3=c3 @ XtX_inv @ c3)

def holm(p2, p3):
    # identical logic to analyze.py / sim_power.py
    r2 = np.zeros_like(p2, bool)
    first2 = p2 <= p3
    pmin = np.minimum(p2, p3); pmax = np.maximum(p2, p3)
    ok1 = pmin <= ALPHA / 2
    ok2 = ok1 & (pmax <= ALPHA)
    # H2 rejected if (it is the min and ok1) or (it is the max and ok2)
    r2 = (first2 & ok1) | (~first2 & ok2)
    return r2

def run(D, sd_within, sd_cell, sd_prompt=0.0, reps=20000, B=1000, seed=1):
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    n, k = D["n"], D["k"]
    ncell = n // k
    npr = D["pid"].max() + 1
    G = sparse.csr_matrix((np.ones(n), (D["pid"], np.arange(n))), shape=(npr, n))
    out = {"hc3": [], "ols": [], "cr_cell": [], "cr_prompt": [], "p2_hc3": [], "d2": [], "se_hc3": [], "se_ols": []}
    done = 0
    while done < reps:
        b = min(B, reps - done)
        y = rng.normal(0, sd_within, (n, b))
        if sd_cell > 0:
            y += rng.normal(0, sd_cell, (ncell, b))[D["cell"]]
        if sd_prompt > 0:
            y += rng.normal(0, sd_prompt, (npr, b))[D["pid"]]
        beta = D["P"] @ y
        res = y - D["X"] @ beta
        d2, d3 = D["c2"] @ beta, D["c3"] @ beta
        w = 1.0 / (1.0 - D["h"]) ** 2
        v2h = (D["a2"] ** 2 * w) @ (res ** 2); v3h = (D["a3"] ** 2 * w) @ (res ** 2)
        s2 = (res ** 2).sum(0) / D["dof"]
        v2o = s2 * D["v2"]; v3o = s2 * D["v3"]
        # cluster-robust by prompt cell (k obs per cell), CR1-type small factor ignored (2880 clusters)
        ra2 = (D["a2"][:, None] * res).reshape(ncell, k, b).sum(1); ra3 = (D["a3"][:, None] * res).reshape(ncell, k, b).sum(1)
        v2c = (ra2 ** 2).sum(0); v3c = (ra3 ** 2).sum(0)
        # cluster-robust by prompt string (shared across models)
        rp2 = G @ (D["a2"][:, None] * res); rp3 = G @ (D["a3"][:, None] * res)
        v2p = (rp2 ** 2).sum(0); v3p = (rp3 ** 2).sum(0)
        for key, (va, vb) in {"hc3": (v2h, v3h), "ols": (v2o, v3o), "cr_cell": (v2c, v3c), "cr_prompt": (v2p, v3p)}.items():
            p2 = stats.t.cdf(d2 / np.sqrt(va), D["dof"]); p3 = 1 - stats.t.cdf(d3 / np.sqrt(vb), D["dof"])
            out[key].append(holm(p2, p3))
            if key == "hc3": out["p2_hc3"].append(p2)
        out["d2"].append(d2); out["se_hc3"].append(np.sqrt(v2h)); out["se_ols"].append(np.sqrt(v2o))
        done += b
    r = {k_: float(np.concatenate(v).mean()) for k_, v in out.items() if k_ in ("hc3", "ols", "cr_cell", "cr_prompt")}
    r["p2_unadj_hc3"] = float((np.concatenate(out["p2_hc3"]) <= ALPHA).mean())
    d2 = np.concatenate(out["d2"]); se = np.concatenate(out["se_hc3"])
    r["sd_true_over_mean_se_hc3"] = float(d2.std() / se.mean())
    r["reps"] = reps
    return r

if __name__ == "__main__":
    t0 = time.time()
    D2 = setup(6, 2)
    print("design:", D2["X"].shape, "rank", np.linalg.matrix_rank(D2["X"]), "dof", D2["dof"], "n clusters (cells)", D2["n"] // 2, "prompts", D2["pid"].max() + 1)
    # cross-check my HC3 against analyze.py's own fit on one simulated data set
    import pandas as pd
    A = L.analyze()
    rng = np.random.default_rng(7)
    y = rng.normal(0, 0.2, D2["n"]) + rng.normal(0, 0.1, D2["n"] // 2)[D2["cell"]]
    rows = D2["rows"]
    grades = {3: "3H", 4: "4H", 5: "5H", 6: "6H", 7: "7H", 8: "8H"}
    df = pd.DataFrame({"model": ["m%d" % i for i in rows[:, 0]], "grade": [grades[i] for i in rows[:, 1]],
                       "label": ["L%d" % (i + 1) for i in rows[:, 2]], "genre": ["story" if i == 0 else "info" for i in rows[:, 3]],
                       "topic": ["t%d" % i for i in rows[:, 4]], "mean_zipf_content": y})
    f = A.fit(df)
    beta = D2["P"] @ y; res = y - D2["X"] @ beta
    my_d2 = D2["c2"] @ beta; my_se = float(np.sqrt(((D2["a2"] ** 2) / (1 - D2["h"]) ** 2) @ res ** 2))
    print("cross-check vs analyze.fit: delta  mine=%.6f analyze=%.6f | HC3 se mine=%.6f analyze=%.6f" % (my_d2, f["h2_delta_zipf"], my_se, f["h2_se"]))
    assert abs(my_d2 - f["h2_delta_zipf"]) < 1e-9 and abs(my_se - f["h2_se"]) < 1e-7, "HC3 cross-check failed"

    scen = [
        ("S0 k=2, no cell effect (baseline)", 2, 0.20, 0.0, 0.0),
        ("S1 k=2, cell SD = 0.5 x within SD (0.10 vs 0.20; ICC 0.20)", 2, 0.20, 0.10, 0.0),
        ("S2 k=2, cell SD = 0.5 x total SD (0.10; within 0.173; ICC 0.25)", 2, float(np.sqrt(0.2 ** 2 - 0.1 ** 2)), 0.10, 0.0),
        ("S3 k=1, same total variance as S1 (nothing to cluster)", 1, float(np.sqrt(0.2 ** 2 + 0.1 ** 2)), 0.0, 0.0),
        ("S4 k=4, cell SD 0.10, within 0.20", 4, 0.20, 0.10, 0.0),
        ("S5 k=2, prompt effect SD 0.10 shared by the 6 models (+ within 0.20)", 2, 0.20, 0.0, 0.10),
    ]
    Ds = {1: setup(6, 1), 2: D2, 4: setup(6, 4)}
    for name, k, sw, sc, sp in scen:
        r = run(Ds[k], sw, sc, sp, reps=20000, seed=100 + k)
        print(f"{name}\n    H2 rejection (Holm, one-sided, a=.05): HC3 text-level {r['hc3']:.3f} | classical OLS {r['ols']:.3f} | cluster-robust by cell {r['cr_cell']:.3f} | by prompt string {r['cr_prompt']:.3f} || unadjusted p2<=.05 (HC3) {r['p2_unadj_hc3']:.3f} | true SD / mean HC3 SE = {r['sd_true_over_mean_se_hc3']:.3f}")
    print("elapsed %.0fs" % (time.time() - t0))

===== FILE readers/tmp_refute/d17.py | 3071 bytes | sha256 b1e421a285e455c8c1789a07ff9e9a71252d25751b09272d7ea49a9cabb6f3b8 =====
"""D17: analyze.py's years conversion (pooled contrast / mean of significant slopes) vs the per-model conversion in the dossier
(2.6: 'ecart divise par la pente de l'etalon du modele' ; AsPredicted 5: 'difference divided by the model's L5 slope')."""
import sys, warnings
import numpy as np, pandas as pd
import statsmodels.formula.api as smf
warnings.filterwarnings("ignore")
sys.path.insert(0, "/tmp/refute")
import loadmods as L
A = L.analyze()

def simulate(slopes, shift_years, seed, sigma=0.2, k=2):
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed); rows = []; topics = [f"t{i}" for i in range(8)]
    for mi, b in enumerate(slopes):
        m = f"m{mi}"; a = rng.normal(4.8, 0.15); topic_eff = dict(zip(topics, rng.normal(0, 0.1, 8)))
        for g, age in A.AGE_MID.items():
            for lab in ["L1", "L2", "L3", "L4", "L5"]:
                shift = {"L1": shift_years, "L2": shift_years / 2}.get(lab, 0.0)
                for ge in ["story", "info"]:
                    for t in topics:
                        for _ in range(k):
                            rows.append((m, g, lab, ge, t, a + b * (age + shift - 9) + topic_eff[t] + (0.05 if ge == "info" else 0) + rng.normal(0, sigma)))
    return pd.DataFrame(rows, columns=["model", "grade", "label", "genre", "topic", "mean_zipf_content"])

def per_model_contrasts(df):
    """per-model L1-L5 estimate, same design matrix as analyze.fit"""
    df = df.copy(); df["mg"] = df["model"] + "_" + df["grade"]; models = sorted(df["model"].unique()); dm = []
    for m in models:
        for lab in ["L1", "L2", "L3", "L4"]:
            c = f"lab_{lab}_{m}"; df[c] = ((df["label"] == lab) & (df["model"] == m)).astype(float); dm.append(c)
    r = smf.ols("mean_zipf_content ~ C(mg) + " + " + ".join(dm) + " + C(genre) + C(topic) - 1", data=df).fit()
    return {m: float(r.params[f"lab_L1_{m}"]) for m in models}

slopes_true = [-0.06, -0.05, -0.04, -0.03, -0.01, -0.005]    # heterogeneous rulers; every model has a true 1.0-year shift
print("true ruler slopes:", slopes_true, "| true shift = 1.0 year in every model (contrast_m = slope_m x 1)")
pooled, permodel_mean, nsig = [], [], []
for seed in range(60):
    df = simulate(slopes_true, 1.0, seed)
    out = A.fit(df)
    c = per_model_contrasts(df)
    yrs = []
    for m, v in out["h1_slopes"].items():
        if v["p_one_sided"] <= A.ALPHA:
            yrs.append(c[m] / v["slope"])                  # dossier: this model's contrast / this model's ruler slope, significant rulers only
    pooled.append(out["h2_shift_years"]); permodel_mean.append(np.mean(yrs) if yrs else np.nan); nsig.append(out["h1_models_with_significant_slope"])
print("analyze.py  h2_shift_years (pooled contrast / mean of significant slopes): mean %.2f  (sd %.2f) over 60 simulated studies" % (np.nanmean(pooled), np.nanstd(pooled)))
print("per-model conversion (mean over models with significant ruler of contrast_m / slope_m): mean %.2f (sd %.2f)" % (np.nanmean(permodel_mean), np.nanstd(permodel_mean)))
print("models with a significant ruler per study: mean %.1f of 6" % np.mean(nsig))

===== FILE readers/tmp_refute/e20_pilot.py | 3874 bytes | sha256 a3634dd5dacdc50ed00dcdc5824d37a4760a44d288c805b2a216d841cb80ebf7 =====
"""E20: what does the 36-text pilot (3 models x 3 grades x 2 labels x 2 genres x 1 topic, k=1) tell us about the L5 age slope?
The slope is estimated from the 6 L5 texts of each model exactly as analyze.py (HC3, one-sided) and pipeline.py (OLS, mean of the 3 slopes) do."""
import sys, json, warnings
import numpy as np, pandas as pd
import statsmodels.formula.api as smf
from scipy import stats
warnings.filterwarnings("ignore")
sys.path.insert(0, "/tmp/refute")
AGE = {"3H": 6.5, "5H": 8.5, "8H": 11.5}
SIGMA, SLOPE_SD = 0.20, 0.01
ages = np.array([6.5, 8.5, 11.5] * 2); genre = np.array([0, 0, 0, 1, 1, 1])
sxx = float(((ages - ages.mean()) ** 2).sum())
print("per-model design: 6 L5 texts, Sxx = %.2f -> analytic SE(slope) = sigma/sqrt(Sxx) = %.4f at sigma=%.2f" % (sxx, SIGMA / np.sqrt(sxx), SIGMA))
print("pooled over the 3 pilot models: SE = %.4f" % (SIGMA / np.sqrt(3 * sxx)))

def one_pilot(rng, b_true):
    sl = []; hc3 = []; p = []; ols_se = []
    for m in range(3):
        bm = b_true + rng.normal(0, SLOPE_SD)
        y = 4.8 + bm * (ages - 9) + 0.05 * genre + rng.normal(0, SIGMA, 6)
        df = pd.DataFrame({"mean_zipf_content": y, "age": ages, "genre": np.where(genre == 0, "story", "info"), "topic": "t0"})
        r = smf.ols("mean_zipf_content ~ age + C(genre) + C(topic)", data=df).fit(cov_type="HC3")   # analyze.py line 80
        b, se = float(r.params["age"]), float(r.bse["age"])
        r0 = smf.ols("mean_zipf_content ~ age + C(genre)", data=df).fit()                           # pipeline.py pilot_summary
        sl.append(float(r0.params["age"])); ols_se.append(float(r0.bse["age"]))
        hc3.append(se); p.append(float(stats.t.cdf(b / se, r.df_resid)))
    return sl, hc3, p, ols_se

rng = np.random.default_rng(2026)
res = {}
for b_true in (-0.04, -0.02):
    N = 3000
    pooled = []; hc3s = []; ps = []; olss = []
    for _ in range(N):
        sl, hc3, p, ols_se = one_pilot(rng, b_true)
        pooled.append(np.mean(sl)); hc3s += hc3; ps += p; olss += ols_se
    pooled = np.array(pooled); ps = np.array(ps).reshape(-1, 3)
    res[b_true] = dict(pooled=pooled)
    print(f"\ntrue mean slope {b_true:+.3f}: pooled pilot slope (mean of 3 per-model OLS slopes, as pipeline.py) mean={pooled.mean():+.4f}, SD={pooled.std():.4f}, 5th-95th pct [{np.percentile(pooled,5):+.3f}, {np.percentile(pooled,95):+.3f}]")
    print(f"   typical estimated per-model SE: HC3 (analyze.py) median={np.median(hc3s):.3f}, mean={np.mean(hc3s):.3f}; classical OLS median={np.median(olss):.3f}")
    print(f"   per-model H1 (one-sided p<=.05, df=3, HC3) rejection rate = {(ps<=0.05).mean():.2f}; P(>=2 of 3 models significant) = {((ps<=0.05).sum(1)>=2).mean():.2f}")

# decision rule of pipeline.pilot_summary: smallest k in (2,3,4) with power_h2 >= .90 at the pooled slope (min(slope,-0.005)), else 4
g = json.load(open("/tmp/refute/e20_grid.json")); sl = np.array(g["slopes"])
order = np.argsort(sl)
def power(k, b):
    return float(np.interp(b, sl[order], np.array(g["grid"][str(k)])[order]))
def choose(b):
    b = min(b, -0.005)
    for k in (2, 3, 4):
        if power(k, b) >= 0.90: return k, True
    return 4, False
for b_true in (-0.04, -0.03, -0.02):
    rng = np.random.default_rng(5)
    pooled = np.array([np.mean([ (b_true + rng.normal(0, SLOPE_SD)) + rng.normal(0, SIGMA / np.sqrt(sxx)) for _ in range(3)]) for _ in range(20000)])
    ks = [choose(b)[0] for b in pooled]
    fallback = np.mean([not choose(b)[1] for b in pooled])
    need = choose(b_true)
    print(f"true slope {b_true:+.3f} (k really needed: {need[0]}{'' if need[1] else ' - none reaches .90'}):  pilot picks k=2 {np.mean(np.array(ks)==2):.2f}, k=3 {np.mean(np.array(ks)==3):.2f}, k=4 {np.mean(np.array(ks)==4):.2f}  (of which silent fallback 'none reaches .90' = {fallback:.2f}); true power of the k it picks, averaged = {np.mean([power(k, b_true) for k in ks]):.2f}")

import sys
sys.dont_write_bytecode = True
import importlib.util, sys
def load(name, path):
    spec = importlib.util.spec_from_file_location(name, path)
    mod = importlib.util.module_from_spec(spec)
    sys.modules[name] = mod
    spec.loader.exec_module(mod)
    return mod

"""Alternative scorer used only to quantify the referee's points. Not part of the study code.
With every fix switched off it must reproduce measures.py exactly (checked in calib.py)."""
import math, re, sys
import pandas as pd
sys.path.insert(0, "/tmp/refute")
import loadmods as L
M = L.measures()

_df = pd.read_csv(M.LEXIQUE_PATH, sep="\t", encoding="latin-1", keep_default_na=False, dtype=str,
                  usecols=["1_ortho", "3_lemme", "4_cgram", "7_freqlemfilms2", "9_freqfilms2"])
_df["ff"] = _df["9_freqfilms2"].str.replace(",", ".").astype(float)
_df["lf"] = _df["7_freqlemfilms2"].str.replace(",", ".").astype(float)
_df = _df.sort_values("ff", ascending=False)
# same aggregation as measures.lexicon(): form freq summed over readings, cgram of the most frequent reading
LEX = _df.groupby("1_ortho").agg(ff=("ff", "sum"), cgram=("4_cgram", "first"), lemme=("3_lemme", "first"), lf=("lf", "first"))

CONTENT = set(M.CONTENT_CGRAMS)
ELIS = dict(M.ELISIONS)
PRON = {"il","elle","on","ils","elles","je","tu","nous","vous","ce","moi","toi","lui","leur","le","la","les","y","en","t"}
def z(f): return math.log10(max(f, 0.01)) + 3

def tokens(text, *, fix_apos=False, fix_hyph=False, fix_aujourdhui=False, names=()):
    names = {n.lower() for n in names}
    out = []
    for s in [s.strip() for s in M.SENT_RE.findall(text) if s.strip()]:
        if fix_apos:
            s = s.replace("’", "'")
        ms = list(M.WORD_RE.finditer(s))          # the very regex of measures.py
        i = k = 0
        while k < len(ms):
            w = ms[k].group(0)
            proper = (i > 0 and w[0].isupper()) or (w[0].isupper() and w.lower() in names)
            low = w.lower()
            if low.endswith("'"):
                merged = False
                if fix_aujourdhui and k + 1 < len(ms) and (low + ms[k+1].group(0).lower()) in LEX.index:
                    low = low + ms[k+1].group(0).lower(); k += 1; merged = True
                if not merged:
                    low = ELIS.get(low[:-1], low[:-1])
            parts = [low]
            if fix_hyph and "-" in low and low not in LEX.index:
                parts = [p for p in low.split("-") if p and p not in PRON]
            for p in parts:
                out.append((p, proper))
            i += 1; k += 1
    return out

def mean_zipf(text, *, freq="form", stop=None, **kw):
    vals = []
    for w, proper in tokens(text, **kw):
        if proper: continue
        if w in LEX.index:
            r = LEX.loc[w]
            if r["cgram"] in CONTENT:
                if stop and (w in stop or r["lemme"] in stop):
                    continue
                vals.append(z(r["ff"] if freq == "form" else r["lf"]))
        else:
            vals.append(z(0.0))
    return (sum(vals) / len(vals) if vals else float("nan")), len(vals)

===== FILE readers/v03_check/scripts/cmp_texts.py | 1849 bytes | sha256 77192d2733c302d6c30168010e3329bd6295cee2a47dbbb9ffae47013b1e3afc =====
import sys, glob, os, re, importlib.util
SP = "/tmp/claude-0/-home-claude/d28672eb-31f4-533e-85fc-00a96d822022/scratchpad"
def load(name, path):
    spec = importlib.util.spec_from_file_location(name, path); m = importlib.util.module_from_spec(spec); spec.loader.exec_module(m); return m
c3 = load("c3", SP + "/v03_check/art5/compute.py")
c2 = load("c2", SP + "/art5/v02_frozen/compute.py")
LEX = "/usr/local/lib/python3.13/dist-packages/pylexique/Lexique383/Lexique383.txt"
lex3, _ = c3.load_lexicon(LEX)
lex2, _ = c2.load_lexicon(LEX)
RESID = {"tout","autre","même","quoi","peu"}
def mean_without(lines, lex, drop_lemmas):
    sents, rows = c3.analyse(lines, lex)
    vals = []
    for t, low, nm in rows:
        if nm or low in c3.ELIDED: continue
        e = lex.get(low)
        if e is None: vals.append(c3.zipf(0)); continue
        cat, lemma, lf, _, _ = e
        if cat in c3.CONTENT and lemma not in c3.EXCLUDED_LEMMAS and lemma not in drop_lemmas:
            vals.append(c3.zipf(lf))
    return sum(vals)/len(vals), len(vals)
print(f"{'text':18s} {'v02 mean/n':>16s} {'v03 mean/n':>16s} {'d(v03-v02)':>10s} {'v03 -tout/autre/meme/quoi/peu':>30s}")
for f in sorted(glob.glob(SP + "/refute_work/texts/*.txt")):
    txt = open(f, encoding="utf-8").read()
    t2, l2 = c2.clean(txt); m2, lem2 = c2.measure(l2, lex2)
    t3, l3 = c3.clean(txt); m3, lem3 = c3.measure(l3, lex3)
    a, n = mean_without(l3, lex3, {"tout","autre","même"})
    b, n2 = mean_without(l3, lex3, RESID)
    print(f"{os.path.basename(f):18s} {m2['mean_zipf_content']:.3f}/{m2['n_content']:3d}      {m3['mean_zipf_content']:.3f}/{m3['n_content']:3d}      {m3['mean_zipf_content']-m2['mean_zipf_content']:+.3f}     tout/autre/même: {a:.3f}/{n} ({a-m3['mean_zipf_content']:+.3f})  +quoi/peu: {b:.3f} ({b-m3['mean_zipf_content']:+.3f})  title v02={t2!r} v03={t3!r}")

===== FILE readers/v03_check/scripts/fake_main.py | 2329 bytes | sha256 b024541c82cc3a551249232b3e5c5022b5ba7756910cf67bfe393326a0df3ffe =====
import json, random, hashlib
random.seed(11)
calls = [json.loads(l) for l in open("calls.jsonl", encoding="utf-8")]
easy = "le chat la maison un chien joue dans le jardin avec sa soeur et son frère il mange une pomme rouge elle regarde le ciel bleu petit grand maman papa enfant jour nuit eau arbre fleur oiseau voit aime court dort".split()
hard = "la croûte terrestre le magma s'échappe lentement des scientifiques surveillent ces phénomènes géologiques grâce à des instruments précis atmosphère condensation évaporation volcanologue sismographe cratère éruption".split()
AGE = {"3H":6.5,"4H":7.5,"5H":8.5,"6H":9.5,"7H":10.5,"8H":11.5}
FR = {"CP":"3H","CE1":"4H","CE2":"5H","CM1":"6H","CM2":"7H","6e":"8H"}
topic_eff = {}
out = open("data/raw/calls_fake.jsonl", "w", encoding="utf-8")
for c in calls:
    if c["kind"] == "text":
        age = AGE[c["grade"]] if c["grade"] != "nograde" else 9.0
        shift = {"L1": 0.9, "L1P": 0.9}.get(c["label"], 0.0)   # planted: bare labels read about one year older
        a = age + shift
        te = topic_eff.setdefault(c["topic"], random.gauss(0, 0.03))
        ph = min(max(0.05 + 0.07*(a-6.5) + te + random.gauss(0, 0.04), 0), 0.9)
        n = max(int(random.gauss(60 + 25*(a-6.5), 15)), 25)
        toks = [random.choice(hard) if random.random() < ph else random.choice(easy) for _ in range(n)]
        sents, i = [], 0
        while i < len(toks):
            k = random.randint(6, 14); s = " ".join(toks[i:i+k]); sents.append(s[0].upper() + s[1:] + "."); i += k
        text = "Un titre court\n\n" + " ".join(sents)
        if random.random() < 0.01: text = "Je suis désolé, mais je ne peux pas écrire ce texte pour des élèves de cet âge. Je vous invite à consulter un enseignant."
    else:
        text = f"Les élèves de {c['grade']} ont entre {c['truth_age_from']} et {c['truth_age_to']} ans."
    rec = dict(c, provider="x", model_requested="x", model_returned="x-2026", sampling={"temperature": 1.0}, status=200, ok=True,
               finish_reason="stop", text=text, input_tokens=50, output_tokens=len(text)//4, cost_usd=0.0, latency_s=1.0,
               utc_start="t", utc="t", text_sha256=hashlib.sha256(text.encode()).hexdigest(), response={})
    out.write(json.dumps(rec, ensure_ascii=False) + "\n")
out.close(); print("wrote", len(calls))

===== FILE readers/v03_check/scripts/fake_pilot_v03.py | 2043 bytes | sha256 43497b477b4bff5ee6df3260186c3fcce8bc8a924567c977d5844e3c0f06d505 =====
import json, random, hashlib, sys
random.seed(7)
calls = [json.loads(l) for l in open("calls_pilot.jsonl", encoding="utf-8")]
easy = "le chat la maison un chien joue dans le jardin avec sa soeur et son frère il mange une pomme rouge elle regarde le ciel bleu petit grand maman papa enfant jour nuit eau arbre fleur oiseau voit aime court dort".split()
hard = "la croûte terrestre le magma s'échappe lentement des scientifiques surveillent ces phénomènes géologiques grâce à des instruments précis atmosphère condensation évaporation volcanologue sismographe cratère éruption".split()
SLOPE_P = float(sys.argv[1]) if len(sys.argv) > 1 else 0.07   # share of hard words added per year
out = open("data/raw/calls_pilot_fake.jsonl", "w", encoding="utf-8")
for c in calls:
    if c["kind"] == "text":
        age = {"3H":6.5,"4H":7.5,"5H":8.5,"6H":9.5,"7H":10.5,"8H":11.5}[c["grade"]]
        ph = min(max(0.05 + SLOPE_P*(age-6.5) + random.gauss(0, 0.04), 0), 0.9)
        n = max(int(random.gauss(60 + 25*(age-6.5), 15)), 25)
        toks = [random.choice(hard) if random.random() < ph else random.choice(easy) for _ in range(n)]
        body = " ".join(toks)
        sents = []
        i = 0
        while i < len(toks):
            k = random.randint(6, 14)
            s = " ".join(toks[i:i+k]); sents.append(s[0].upper() + s[1:] + ".")
            i += k
        text = "Un titre court\n\n" + " ".join(sents)
        finish = "stop"
    else:
        text = f"Les élèves de {c['grade']} ont entre {c['truth_age_from']} et {c['truth_age_to']} ans."
        finish = "stop"
    rec = dict(c, provider="x", model_requested="x", model_returned="x-2026", sampling={"temperature": 1.0}, status=200, ok=True,
               finish_reason=finish, text=text, input_tokens=50, output_tokens=len(text)//4, cost_usd=0.0, latency_s=1.0,
               utc_start="t", utc="t", text_sha256=hashlib.sha256(text.encode()).hexdigest(), response={})
    out.write(json.dumps(rec, ensure_ascii=False) + "\n")
out.close()
print("wrote", len(calls))

import sys, random, time, importlib.util

random.seed(5)
pool = list("abcdefghijklmnopqrstuvwxyzéèêàçœæÉŒ ,.;:!?…«»\"“”‘’'—–-#*_()0123456789\n\t  ") + ["Le ", "la ", "Désolé", "Titre :", "Texte", "**", "## ", "l'", "d'", "dit-il", "grand-mère", "aujourd'hui", "🙂", "​"]
t0 = time.time(); n = 0; errs = 0

    s = "".join(random.choice(pool) for _ in range(random.randint(0, 300)))
    try:
        title, lines = c3.clean(s)
        m, lem = c3.measure(lines, lex)
        r = c3.exclusion_reason({"ok": True, "finish_reason": "stop", "text": s}, m)
        n += 1
    except Exception as e:
        errs += 1; print("ERR", repr(s[:80]), type(e).__name__, e)
        if errs > 5: break
print("fuzz ok", n, "errors", errs, "time", round(time.time() - t0, 1), "s")
big = ("Désolé " * 20000) + "\n" + ("Le chat dort. " * 20000)
t0 = time.time(); c3.exclusion_reason({"ok": True, "finish_reason": "stop", "text": big}, {"n_words": 100, "share_oov": 0}); print("regex on 400k chars:", round(time.time() - t0, 3), "s")

t0 = time.time(); c3.REFUSAL_RE.match(big2.strip()); print("regex whitespace-heavy:", round(time.time() - t0, 3), "s")

body = """Le corps humain
Notre corps est une machine très complexe. Le cœur est un muscle qui pompe le sang dans tout le corps. Il bat environ cent fois par minute. Les poumons nous permettent de respirer. L'œil capte la lumière et envoie des messages au cerveau. Nos os forment un squelette solide. Les muscles bougent nos bras et nos jambes. Pour rester en bonne santé, il faut bien manger, bien dormir et faire du sport chaque jour."""
ferme = """La ferme de Léo
Léo habite dans une ferme avec sa sœur Lola. Chaque matin, ils ramassent les œufs dans le poulailler. Les poules caquettent dans la cour. Le fermier donne du foin aux vaches et aux chevaux. Léo aime beaucoup les animaux. Le soir, la famille mange une grande soupe de légumes. Lola a un petit mouton qu'elle appelle Flocon."""

    print(f"{n}: official-like lexicon mean {a['mean_zipf_content']:.3f} oov {a['share_oov']:.3f} | ligature-spelled lexicon mean {b['mean_zipf_content']:.3f} oov {b['share_oov']:.3f}  (delta {b['mean_zipf_content']-a['mean_zipf_content']:+.3f})")
# leaked clarification vs typical story

# tout/autre tokens in sample texts

for f in sorted(glob.glob(SP + "/refute_work/texts/*.txt")):
    _, lines = c3.clean(open(f, encoding="utf-8").read())
    _, rows = c3.analyse(lines, lex)
    hits = [low for t, low, nm in rows if not nm and low in lex and lex[low][1] in ("tout", "autre", "même") and lex[low][0] in c3.CONTENT]
    m = c3.measure(lines, lex)[0]
    tot += len(hits)
    print(os.path.basename(f), hits, "of", m["n_content"], "content tokens")
print("total", tot)

===== FILE readers/v03_check/scripts/n11_driver.py | 3793 bytes | sha256 756a10549f590772887f6eb23d1ec0c712910d924974295f34cf10e7cdb441bb =====
import json, os, sys, threading, http.server, socketserver, collections
os.environ["NO_PROXY"] = "127.0.0.1,localhost"; os.environ["no_proxy"] = "127.0.0.1,localhost"
for k in ("HTTP_PROXY","http_proxy","HTTPS_PROXY","https_proxy"): os.environ.pop(k, None)
GOOD = ("Le petit renard marche dans la forêt. Il voit un grand arbre et une petite maison. Il a faim, alors il cherche des pommes rouges "
        "pour son repas. Le soir, il rentre chez lui et il dort bien dans son lit chaud.")
REFUSAL = ("Je suis désolé, mais je ne peux pas écrire ce texte pour des élèves de cet âge. Je vous invite à consulter un enseignant ou un manuel "
           "scolaire pour obtenir des textes adaptés à votre classe.")
MERCI = ("Merci pour votre demande. Pour écrire un texte adapté, pouvez-vous préciser ce que signifie 5H ? S'agit-il d'une classe de la "
         "cinquième année primaire, et quel âge ont les élèves ? Dès que vous m'aurez répondu, je rédigerai le titre et le texte.")
seen = collections.Counter()
class H(http.server.BaseHTTPRequestHandler):
    def log_message(self, *a): pass
    def do_POST(self):
        n = int(self.headers.get("content-length", 0)); body = json.loads(self.rfile.read(n))
        prompt = body["messages"][0]["content"]; seen[prompt] += 1; k = seen[prompt]
        def reply(status, obj):
            data = json.dumps(obj).encode(); self.send_response(status); self.send_header("content-type", "application/json")
            self.send_header("content-length", str(len(data))); self.end_headers(); self.wfile.write(data)
        def ok(text, finish="stop"):
            reply(200, {"model": "gpt-test-2026", "choices": [{"message": {"content": text}, "finish_reason": finish}], "usage": {"prompt_tokens": 20, "completion_tokens": 60}})
        if "FAIL_ONCE" in prompt:
            return reply(422, {"error": "bad request"}) if k == 1 else ok("Titre court\n\n" + GOOD)
        if "EMPTY_ONCE" in prompt:
            return ok("") if k == 1 else ok("Titre court\n\n" + GOOD)
        if "REFUSE_ONCE" in prompt:
            return ok(REFUSAL) if k == 1 else ok("Titre court\n\n" + GOOD)
        if "MERCI_ONCE" in prompt:
            return ok(MERCI) if k == 1 else ok("Titre court\n\n" + GOOD)
        if "ALWAYS_EMPTY" in prompt:
            return ok("", "content_filter")
        if "ALWAYS_422" in prompt:
            return reply(422, {"error": "bad request"})
        return ok("Titre court\n\n" + GOOD)
srv = socketserver.ThreadingTCPServer(("127.0.0.1", 0), H); srv.daemon_threads = True
port = srv.server_address[1]
threading.Thread(target=srv.serve_forever, daemon=True).start()
os.environ["ART5_ENDPOINT_OVERRIDE"] = f"http://127.0.0.1:{port}"; os.environ["OPENAI_API_KEY"] = "dummy"
names = ["NORMAL_1","NORMAL_2","FAIL_ONCE_a","EMPTY_ONCE","REFUSE_ONCE","ALWAYS_EMPTY","ALWAYS_422","FAIL_ONCE_b","NORMAL_3","MERCI_ONCE"]
with open("calls.jsonl", "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
    for i, nme in enumerate(names):
        f.write(json.dumps({"kind":"text","block":"main","model":"openai_balanced","grade":"5H","label":"L1","genre":"story","topic":"t","prompt":f"{nme} Écris une histoire","call_id":f"main-{i:05d}"}) + "\n")
sys.path.insert(0, os.getcwd())
import pipeline, compute
pipeline.run_calls("calls.jsonl", 1.0, 30)
print("=== logs"); 
for fn in sorted(os.listdir("logs")):
    d = json.load(open("logs/" + fn)); print(fn, {k: d[k] for k in ("to_do","launched","ok","failed","stopped")})
print("=== raw files:", sorted(os.listdir("data/raw")))
df = compute.compute(pipeline.LEXIQUE_PATH, "data/raw/*.jsonl", "results/m.csv", prefix="main-")
print(df[["call_id","kept","exclusion_reason","n_words","raw_file"]].assign(raw_file=lambda d: d.raw_file.str.slice(9, 40)).to_string())
print("requests per prompt:", dict(seen))

import sys, warnings
sys.path.insert(0, "/tmp/claude-0/-home-claude/d28672eb-31f4-533e-85fc-00a96d822022/scratchpad/v03_check/art5")
import numpy as np, pandas as pd
import statsmodels.formula.api as smf
from scipy import stats
import analyze as A
import sim_power
warnings.filterwarnings("ignore")
M, G, E, T = 3, 6, 2, 6
idx = np.indices((M, G, E, T)).reshape(4, -1).T
m_, g_, e_, t_ = idx.T
age = A.AGES[g_]
def dummies(v, drop_first=True):
    k = np.unique(v); cols = [(v == x).astype(float) for x in k]
    return np.column_stack(cols[1:] if drop_first else cols)
X_pool = np.column_stack([np.ones(len(age)), age, dummies(m_), dummies(e_), dummies(t_)])
X_cells = np.column_stack([np.ones(len(age)), dummies(m_ * 10 + g_), dummies(e_), dummies(t_)])
def fit_stats(y):
    # pooled HC3
    XtXi = np.linalg.pinv(X_pool.T @ X_pool); beta = XtXi @ X_pool.T @ y
    e = y - X_pool @ beta; h = np.einsum("ij,jk,ik->i", X_pool, XtXi, X_pool)
    meat = (X_pool * (e / (1 - h))[:, None]).T @ (X_pool * (e / (1 - h))[:, None])
    cov = XtXi @ meat @ XtXi
    slope, se = beta[1], np.sqrt(cov[1, 1])
    # cells sigma
    Xc = X_cells; bc = np.linalg.lstsq(Xc, y, rcond=None)[0]; r = y - Xc @ bc
    dfr = len(y) - np.linalg.matrix_rank(Xc)
    sig = np.sqrt(r @ r / dfr)
    return slope, se, sig, dfr
# check against statsmodels once
rng = np.random.default_rng(1)
c = A.simulate_cubes({}, rng, n_models=M, n_topics=T, sigma=0.3, slope=-0.04, labels=["L5"], tau_ps=0.05, missing=0.0)["L5"]
y = c[m_, g_, e_, t_]
d = pd.DataFrame(dict(y=y, age=age, model=m_, genre=e_, topic=t_, grade=g_))
f1 = smf.ols("y ~ age + C(model) + C(genre) + C(topic)", data=d).fit(cov_type="HC3")
f2 = smf.ols("y ~ C(model):C(grade) + C(genre) + C(topic)", data=d).fit()
print("check numpy vs statsmodels:", fit_stats(y), (f1.params["age"], f1.bse["age"], np.sqrt(f2.scale), f2.df_resid))
R = 3000
print("n texts", len(y))
print(f"{'sigma':>5s} {'true':>6s} | P(stop: p>.05) | mean SE | mean sigma_hat/true_resid_sd | P(sigma_upper80 >= true resid sd)")
for sigma in (0.15, 0.2, 0.3, 0.4):
    resid_sd = np.sqrt(sigma**2 + 0.05**2)
    for true in (-0.02, -0.04, -0.06):
        stops, ses, sigs, cover = [], [], [], []
        for _ in range(R):
            c = A.simulate_cubes({}, rng, n_models=M, n_topics=T, sigma=sigma, slope=true, labels=["L5"], tau_ps=0.05, missing=0.0)["L5"]
            y = c[m_, g_, e_, t_]
            sl, se, sg, dfr = fit_stats(y)
            p = stats.norm.cdf(sl / se)
            up = sg * np.sqrt(dfr / stats.chi2.ppf(0.20, dfr))
            stops.append(p > 0.05); ses.append(se); sigs.append(sg); cover.append(up >= resid_sd)
        print(f"{sigma:5.2f} {true:+6.2f} | {np.mean(stops):.3f}          | {np.mean(ses):.4f}  | {np.mean(sigs)/resid_sd:.3f}                        | {np.mean(cover):.3f}")

import sys
sys.dont_write_bytecode = True
import time, pickle, os
ART5 = "/tmp/claude-0/-home-claude/d28672eb-31f4-533e-85fc-00a96d822022/scratchpad/v03_refute/art5"
sys.path.insert(0, ART5)
import compute
LEXPATH = "/usr/local/lib/python3.13/site-packages/pylexique/Lexique383/Lexique383.txt"
CACHE = "/tmp/claude-0/-home-claude/d28672eb-31f4-533e-85fc-00a96d822022/scratchpad/v03_refute/scripts/lex_cache.pkl"

def get_lex():
    if os.path.exists(CACHE):
        return pickle.load(open(CACHE, "rb"))
    t = time.time()
    lex, sha = compute.load_lexicon(LEXPATH)
    pickle.dump((lex, sha), open(CACHE, "wb"))
    print("loaded lexicon in %.1fs, %d forms" % (time.time() - t, len(lex)), file=sys.stderr)
    return lex, sha

===== FILE readers/v03_refute/scripts/d2_make_ligature_lexicon.py | 1027 bytes | sha256 5b272ecb2a096655c525aeaa04cc5cfb78c441b6bc31c13550ccca37b973aaa6 =====
import sys
sys.dont_write_bytecode = True
import re
SRC = "/usr/local/lib/python3.13/site-packages/pylexique/Lexique383/Lexique383.txt"
DST = "/tmp/claude-0/-home-claude/d28672eb-31f4-533e-85fc-00a96d822022/scratchpad/v03_refute/scripts/Lexique383_ligature_copy.tsv"
def lig(w):
    # French orthography: oeu -> oeu ligature (coeur, soeur, oeuvre, oeuf, boeuf, voeu, noeud, manoeuvre), oei (oeil), oes (oesophage)
    return w.replace("oeu", "œu").replace("oei", "œi").replace("oes", "œs").replace("foet", "fœt")
n_ortho = n_lemme = 0
with open(SRC, encoding="latin-1", newline="") as f, open(DST, "w", encoding="utf-8", newline="") as g:
    for i, line in enumerate(f):
        if i == 0:
            g.write(line); continue
        parts = line.rstrip("\n").split("\t")
        a, c = lig(parts[0]), lig(parts[2])
        n_ortho += a != parts[0]; n_lemme += c != parts[2]
        parts[0], parts[2] = a, c
        g.write("\t".join(parts) + "\n")
print("rows with ortho changed:", n_ortho, "| lemme changed:", n_lemme)

import sys
sys.dont_write_bytecode = True
sys.path.insert(0, "/tmp/claude-0/-home-claude/d28672eb-31f4-533e-85fc-00a96d822022/scratchpad/v03_refute/scripts")

def run(label, text):
    title, lines = compute.clean(text)
    m, _ = compute.measure(lines, lex)
    lost = [b for b in body if b not in lines]
    print("%-52s title=%-34r kept_lines=%d  n_words=%3d  meanZ=%.3f  body paragraphs lost: %d" % (label, title[:34], len(lines), m["n_words"], m["mean_zipf_content"], len(lost)))
    return m

===== FILE readers/v03_refute/scripts/d4_corpus_level.py | 1493 bytes | sha256 20d29a5a3dc6ee7feed16989b162050a8bfec6a465c82b8f842e98a50e2d8532 =====
import sys
sys.dont_write_bytecode = True
sys.path.insert(0, "/tmp/claude-0/-home-claude/d28672eb-31f4-533e-85fc-00a96d822022/scratchpad/v03_refute/scripts")

import pandas as pd

df.columns = [c.split("_", 1)[1] for c in df.columns]
for c in ["freqfilms2", "freqlivres"]:
    df[c] = df[c].fillna("0").str.replace(",", ".").astype(float)
tot = df.groupby("ortho")[["freqfilms2", "freqlivres"]].sum()   # token frequency of each written form (per million)
REM = {"tout", "autre", "même"}
for corp in ["freqlivres", "freqfilms2"]:
    num = den = 0.0; rem_w = 0.0; rem_z = 0.0; rem_forms = {}
    for form, (cat, lemma, lemfreq, ff, nb) in lex.items():
        w = tot.at[form, corp] if form in tot.index else 0.0
        if cat in compute.CONTENT and lemma not in compute.EXCLUDED_LEMMAS:
            z = compute.zipf(lemfreq)
            num += w * z; den += w
            if lemma in REM and cat == "ADJ":
                rem_w += w; rem_z += w * z; rem_forms[form] = round(w, 1)
    m = num / den
    zbar = rem_z / rem_w
    share = rem_w / den
    new = (num - rem_z) / (den - rem_w)
    print("%-10s corpus-level mean Zipf of content readings = %.3f | removed forms %s | share of content tokens = %.2f%% | mean Zipf of removed tokens = %.2f | mean after removal = %.3f | delta = %+.3f" % (corp, m, rem_forms, 100 * share, zbar, new, new - m))

import sys
sys.dont_write_bytecode = True

lex, _ = common.get_lex()
def variant(removed_lemmas):
    l2 = dict(lex)
    for f, (cat, lemma, lf, ff, nb) in lex.items():
        if cat == "ADJ" and lemma in removed_lemmas:
            l2[f] = ("ADJ:ind", lemma, lf, ff, nb)
    return l2
lex_rm = variant({"tout", "autre", "même"})      # what D4 proposes to remove (only forms actually read as plain ADJ are affected)
print("forms actually read as plain ADJ with lemma tout/autre/même:", sorted(f for f, e in lex.items() if e[0] == "ADJ" and e[1] in {"tout", "autre", "même"}))
print()
print("%-6s %-3s %-20s %5s %7s %7s %8s %8s" % ("genre", "gr", "topic", "n_cont", "removed", "share%", "meanZ", "delta"))
deltas = []
for genre, gr, topic, txt in TEXTS:
    _, body = compute.clean(txt)
    a, la = compute.measure(body, lex)
    b, lb = compute.measure(body, lex_rm)
    d = b["mean_zipf_content"] - a["mean_zipf_content"]
    rem = a["n_content"] - b["n_content"]
    deltas.append(d)
    print("%-6s %-3s %-20s %5d %7d %7.1f %8.3f %+8.3f   removed: %s" % (genre, gr, topic, a["n_content"], rem, 100 * rem / a["n_content"], a["mean_zipf_content"], d, sorted(set(la) - set(lb)) or ""))
print("\nproxy texts: mean delta = %+.4f  | median = %+.4f | min = %+.3f | max = %+.3f | n = %d" % (statistics.mean(deltas), statistics.median(deltas), min(deltas), max(deltas), len(deltas)))
print("texts with delta <= -0.02: %d of %d" % (sum(d <= -0.02 for d in deltas), len(deltas)))

===== FILE readers/v03_refute/scripts/d5_driver.py | 2791 bytes | sha256 d2fe978e8d4aaff30758baa3ffd0c33c503e37d46ce099594ea17e7b49289291 =====
"""Offline harness for D5: mock provider on localhost; runs pipeline.run_calls exactly as the workflow does."""
import sys
sys.dont_write_bytecode = True
import os, json, glob, threading
from http.server import BaseHTTPRequestHandler, ThreadingHTTPServer
SB = "/tmp/claude-0/-home-claude/d28672eb-31f4-533e-85fc-00a96d822022/scratchpad/v03_refute/scripts/sandbox_d5"
os.chdir(SB); sys.path.insert(0, SB)
for k in list(os.environ):
    if "proxy" in k.lower(): os.environ.pop(k)
os.environ.update({"NO_PROXY": "*", "no_proxy": "*", "PYTHONDONTWRITEBYTECODE": "1", "ANTHROPIC_API_KEY": "dummy"})
seen, lock = {}, threading.Lock()
MODE = sys.argv[1] if len(sys.argv) > 1 else "all_fail_then_short"
class H(BaseHTTPRequestHandler):
    def do_POST(self):
        body = json.loads(self.rfile.read(int(self.headers["content-length"])))
        prompt = body["messages"][0]["content"]
        with lock:
            seen[prompt] = seen.get(prompt, 0) + 1; k = seen[prompt]
        if k == 1: text = ""                       # 200 OK, visible text empty, all 4000 output tokens billed (hidden reasoning)
        elif k == 2: text = "Trop court."          # answer under 20 words -> goes to the retry list
        else: text = " ".join(["Le petit lapin court dans la forêt et regarde le ciel bleu."] * 3)
        resp = {"content": [{"type": "text", "text": text}], "usage": {"input_tokens": 50, "output_tokens": 4000},
                "model": "mock-1", "stop_reason": "end_turn" if text else "max_tokens"}
        data = json.dumps(resp).encode()
        self.send_response(200); self.send_header("content-type", "application/json"); self.send_header("content-length", str(len(data)))
        self.end_headers(); self.wfile.write(data)
    def log_message(self, *a): pass
srv = ThreadingHTTPServer(("127.0.0.1", 0), H)
threading.Thread(target=srv.serve_forever, daemon=True).start()
os.environ["ART5_ENDPOINT_OVERRIDE"] = "http://127.0.0.1:%d" % srv.server_address[1]
import pipeline
CAP = float(sys.argv[2]) if len(sys.argv) > 2 else 1.05
print("cap per generate.py invocation = %.2f USD; worst case per call = 0.10 USD (25 USD/Mtok x 4000 tokens)" % CAP)
pipeline.run_calls("calls_d5.jsonl", CAP, 60)
print("\n=== passes (logs/run_*.json) ===")
tot = 0.0
for p in sorted(glob.glob("logs/run_*.json")):
    s = json.load(open(p)); tot += s["spent_usd"]
    print("%-18s to_do=%2d launched=%2d ok=%2d failed=%2d spent=%.2f cap=%.2f stopped=%s" % (s["calls_file"], s["to_do"], s["launched"], s["ok"], s["failed"], s["spent_usd"], s["cap_usd"], s["stopped"]))
tot_raw = sum(json.loads(l)["cost_usd"] for f in glob.glob("data/raw/*.jsonl") for l in open(f))
print("TOTAL spent in this one step: %.2f USD (sum of cost_usd in raw files %.2f) = %.2fx the cap of %.2f" % (tot, tot_raw, tot / CAP, CAP))

    if "proxy" in k.lower(): os.environ.pop(k)

    def do_POST(self):
        body = json.loads(self.rfile.read(int(self.headers["content-length"])))
        prompt = body["messages"][0]["content"]
        with lock:
            seen[prompt] = seen.get(prompt, 0) + 1; k = seen[prompt]
        if k == 1: text = "Trop court."
        elif k == 2: text = " ".join(["Le petit lapin court dans la forêt et regarde le ciel bleu."] * 3)
        else: text = " ".join(["Le petit lapin court dans la forêt et regarde le ciel bleu."] * 3)

                "model": "mock-1", "stop_reason": "end_turn" if text else "max_tokens"}
        data = json.dumps(resp).encode()
        self.send_response(200); self.send_header("content-type", "application/json"); self.send_header("content-length", str(len(data)))
        self.end_headers(); self.wfile.write(data)
    def log_message(self, *a): pass

import sys
sys.dont_write_bytecode = True
src = open("/tmp/claude-0/-home-claude/d28672eb-31f4-533e-85fc-00a96d822022/scratchpad/v03_refute/art5/compute.py", encoding="utf-8").read()
out = "/tmp/claude-0/-home-claude/d28672eb-31f4-533e-85fc-00a96d822022/scratchpad/v03_refute/scripts/mutants/"
def mut(name, pairs):
    s = src
    for a, b in pairs:
        assert s.count(a) == 1, (name, a, s.count(a))
        s = s.replace(a, b)
    open(out + f"compute_{name}.py", "w", encoding="utf-8").write(s)

MATCH = "REFUSAL_RE.match(re.sub"
mut("M0_original", [])
mut("M1_match_to_search", [(MATCH, "REFUSAL_RE.search(re.sub")])
mut("M2_unanchored_search", [(MATCH, "REFUSAL_RE.search(re.sub"), ('r"^\\W*(je suis désolée?|', 'r"(je suis désolée?|')])
mut("M3_drop_caret_keep_match", [('r"^\\W*(je suis désolée?|', 'r"(je suis désolée?|')])
mut("M4_no_ignorecase", [("r\"can you clarify)\", re.IGNORECASE)", "r\"can you clarify)\")")])
mut("M5_no_text_check", [("or REFUSAL_RE.match(re.sub(r\"[#*_]+\", \" \", normalise(rec.get(\"text\", \"\"))).strip())", "")])

===== FILE readers/v041_check/scripts/fast/sitecustomize.py | 374 bytes | sha256 c71cde0a6b54d315436038f79d12b61c49ababcf89ba562c032e2ebc3ee6cd09 =====
"""Test helper only: shortens the retry sleeps of generate.py (5 to 160 s) so that stress runs finish.
Loaded through PYTHONPATH by n1_run.py when --fast is given. The budget logic does not depend on the duration of a sleep."""
import os
import time

_real = time.sleep


def _fast(s):
    _real(min(s, float(os.environ.get("FAST_SLEEP_MAX", "0.02"))))


time.sleep = _fast

===== FILE readers/v041_check/scripts/lib41.py | 1800 bytes | sha256 9ac800fe04a1b7390b05065cc9688a2e62f2ece231f6da5fb195d39988c02d1d =====
# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
"""Loader for the v0.4.1 check. Imports compute.py from a code folder (never edits it), caches the lexicon.
load("v041") -> the code under test; load("v04") -> the previous version, for before/after comparison."""
import importlib.util
import os
import pickle
import sys

ROOT = "/tmp/claude-0/-home-claude/d28672eb-31f4-533e-85fc-00a96d822022/scratchpad"
DIRS = {"v041": ROOT + "/v041_check/art5", "v04": ROOT + "/v04_check/art5", "v042": ROOT + "/v041_check/art5_v042", "v043": ROOT + "/v041_check/art5_v043", "v044": ROOT + "/v041_check/art5_v044"}
LEXPATH = "/usr/local/lib/python3.13/site-packages/pylexique/Lexique383/Lexique383.txt"
CACHE = ROOT + "/v041_check/scripts/lex_v041.pkl"
sys.dont_write_bytecode = True

_mods = {}


def load(which="v041"):
    if which in _mods:
        return _mods[which]
    d = DIRS[which]
    spec = importlib.util.spec_from_file_location("compute_" + which, d + "/compute.py")
    mod = importlib.util.module_from_spec(spec)
    spec.loader.exec_module(mod)
    if os.path.exists(CACHE):
        lex = pickle.load(open(CACHE, "rb"))
    else:
        lex, _ = mod.load_lexicon(LEXPATH)
        pickle.dump(lex, open(CACHE, "wb"))
    _mods[which] = (mod, lex)
    return mod, lex


def verdict(mod, lex, text, finish="stop"):
    """What the pipeline does with one answer: excluded reason, flag_meta, meta lines removed, title, n_words."""
    info = mod.clean_info(text)
    m, _ = mod.measure(info["lines"], lex)
    rec = {"ok": True, "finish_reason": finish, "text": text}
    reason = mod.exclusion_reason(rec, m)
    return {"excl": reason, "flag": mod.flag_meta(text), "n_meta": info["n_meta_lines"], "title": info["title"],
            "lines": info["lines"], "words": m.get("n_words", 0), "mean": m.get("mean_zipf_content")}

===== FILE readers/v041_check/scripts/mock_logged.py | 6055 bytes | sha256 8721a1fd7c413f692e388cf0e3059c75c532259255ca9f6136229aeddb2fcc74 =====
"""Instrumented copy of exp/mock_bad.py for the v0.4.1 check (the original is not modified).
Same behaviour as mock_bad.py (requests 5 and 30 get a 200 response cut in the middle of the body, request 3 gets a 429,
the openai route rejects temperature, 3,800 output tokens are reported for every answer), plus:
  MOCK_LOG   path of a JSONL file: one line per request that reached the handler, with what the provider would have billed
             (every request that produced an answer, also one whose body was then cut, is billed 40 in + 3,800 out tokens)
  CUT_PROB   probability (0..1) that a further 200 response is cut in the middle of the body (stress test, default 0)
  CUT_ORIG   '0' removes the two cuts of the original (default '1')
  DELAY      seconds of latency before answering (default 0)
Not data."""
import json, os, random, re, sys, threading, time
from http.server import ThreadingHTTPServer, BaseHTTPRequestHandler

EASY = "chat chien maison jardin soleil pain eau arbre fleur école ami jouer manger courir voir petit grand beau content".split()
HARD = "insecte pollen colonie nectar ruche éruption magma cratère migration altitude hémisphère photosynthèse écosystème".split()
COUNT = {"n": 0}
SEEN = set()
LOCK = threading.Lock()
LOG = os.environ.get("MOCK_LOG")
CUT_PROB = float(os.environ.get("CUT_PROB", "0"))
CUT_ORIG = os.environ.get("CUT_ORIG", "1") == "1"
DELAY = float(os.environ.get("DELAY", "0"))
CUT_RNG = random.Random(12345)
TOKENS = {"in": 40, "out": 3800}


def log(path, model, event, billed):
    if not LOG:
        return
    with LOCK:
        with open(LOG, "a", encoding="utf-8") as f:
            f.write(json.dumps({"t": time.time(), "route": path, "model": model, "event": event,
                                "in": TOKENS["in"] if billed else 0, "out": TOKENS["out"] if billed else 0}) + "\n")


def age_of(prompt):
    m = re.search(r"de (\d+) à (\d+) ans", prompt)
    if m:
        return (int(m.group(1)) + int(m.group(2))) / 2
    m = re.search(r"de (\d)[HP]\b", prompt)
    if m:
        return int(m.group(1)) + 3.5
    return 9.0


def make_text(prompt, rng):
    if prompt.startswith("À quel âge") or "quel âge" in prompt:
        g = re.search(r"(\d)[HP]", prompt)
        n = int(g.group(1)) if g else 5
        return f"Les élèves de {n}H ont en général entre {n + 3} et {n + 4} ans.", "stop"
    a = age_of(prompt)
    p_hard = min(max((a - 5) / 12, 0.05), 0.6)
    n = rng.randint(60, 160)
    words = [rng.choice(HARD) if rng.random() < p_hard else rng.choice(EASY) for _ in range(n)]
    sents, i = [], 0
    while i < len(words):
        k = rng.randint(6, 12)
        s = " ".join(words[i:i + k])
        sents.append("Le " + s + ".")
        i += k
    return "Le jardin des abeilles\n\n" + " ".join(sents), "stop"


class H(BaseHTTPRequestHandler):
    def log_message(self, *a):
        pass

    def do_POST(self):
        body = json.loads(self.rfile.read(int(self.headers["Content-Length"])).decode())
        prompt = body["messages"][0]["content"]
        route = self.path.strip("/")
        model = body["model"]
        if route == "openai" and "temperature" in body:  # this mock model refuses the parameter
            msg = b'{"error": {"message": "Unsupported parameter: temperature is not supported with this model."}}'
            log(route, model, "400_temperature", False)
            self.send_response(400); self.send_header("Content-Length", str(len(msg))); self.end_headers(); self.wfile.write(msg); return
        rng = random.Random(prompt + model)
        with LOCK:
            COUNT["n"] += 1
            n = COUNT["n"]
            extra_cut = CUT_PROB > 0 and CUT_RNG.random() < CUT_PROB
        if DELAY:
            time.sleep(DELAY)
        if (CUT_ORIG and n in (5, 30)) or extra_cut:  # connection drops in the middle of a 200 response body
            log(route, model, "cut_midbody", True)
            self.send_response(200); self.send_header("Content-Length", "5000"); self.end_headers()
            self.wfile.write(b'{"model": "x", "choices": ['); self.wfile.flush(); self.close_connection = True; return
        if n == 3:  # one transient rate-limit error
            log(route, model, "429", False)
            self.send_response(429); self.end_headers(); self.wfile.write(b'{"error":"rate"}'); return
        text, finish = make_text(prompt, rng)
        key = prompt + model
        with LOCK:
            first = key not in SEEN
            SEEN.add(key)
        if first and rng.random() < 0.06:
            text, finish = "Voici un texte court.", "stop"  # too short on the first request only
        if first and rng.random() < 0.04:  # a transport failure on the first request only
            msg = b'{"error": "bad gateway at the mock"}'
            log(route, model, "400_badgateway", False)
            self.send_response(400); self.send_header("Content-Length", str(len(msg))); self.end_headers(); self.wfile.write(msg); return
        if rng.random() < 0.02:
            finish = "length"
        if route == "anthropic":
            out = {"model": model + "-20261001", "content": [{"type": "text", "text": text}],
                   "stop_reason": "end_turn" if finish == "stop" else "max_tokens",
                   "usage": {"input_tokens": 40, "output_tokens": 3800}}
        else:
            out = {"model": model, "choices": [{"message": {"content": text}, "finish_reason": finish}],
                   "usage": {"prompt_tokens": 40, "completion_tokens": 3800}}
        data = json.dumps(out).encode()
        log(route, model, "ok", True)
        self.send_response(200); self.send_header("Content-Type", "application/json"); self.send_header("Content-Length", str(len(data))); self.end_headers(); self.wfile.write(data)

    def do_GET(self):
        data = b'{"data": []}'
        self.send_response(200); self.send_header("Content-Length", str(len(data))); self.end_headers(); self.wfile.write(data)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    ThreadingHTTPServer(("127.0.0.1", int(sys.argv[1])), H).serve_forever()

===== FILE readers/v041_check/scripts/mock_weird.py | 3436 bytes | sha256 0449f862823977c1a11e2a86cfff83fae1bf3556f472856bf05e53ab887ce766 =====
"""Offline stand-in for the provider APIs that answers the K-th request of a route with a malformed-but-HTTP-200 body (WEIRD shape),
and every other request normally. For the v0.4.1 check of 'one() never lets an error stop the run'. Not data.
env: WEIRD shape name, WEIRD_K (default 2): index (1-based) of the request that gets the weird body."""
import json, os, sys, threading
from http.server import ThreadingHTTPServer, BaseHTTPRequestHandler
SHAPE = os.environ.get("WEIRD", "none"); K = int(os.environ.get("WEIRD_K", "2"))
N = {}; LOCK = threading.Lock()
TEXT = "Le jardin des abeilles\n\n" + " ".join(["Le chat joue dans le jardin avec un petit chien."] * 6)

def normal(route, model):
    if route == "anthropic":
        return {"model": model, "content": [{"type": "text", "text": TEXT}], "stop_reason": "end_turn", "usage": {"input_tokens": 40, "output_tokens": 300}}
    return {"model": model, "choices": [{"message": {"content": TEXT}, "finish_reason": "stop"}], "usage": {"prompt_tokens": 40, "completion_tokens": 300}}

def weird(route, model, shape):
    b = normal(route, model)
    if shape == "list": return json.dumps([1, 2, 3]).encode()
    if shape == "empty_choices":
        return json.dumps({"content": [], "usage": {"input_tokens": 40, "output_tokens": 0}} if route == "anthropic" else {"choices": [], "usage": {"prompt_tokens": 40, "completion_tokens": 0}}).encode()
    if shape == "usage_null": b["usage"] = None
    if shape == "tokens_null": b["usage"] = {"input_tokens": None, "output_tokens": None} if route == "anthropic" else {"prompt_tokens": None, "completion_tokens": None}
    if shape == "content_number":
        if route == "anthropic": b["content"][0]["text"] = 5
        else: b["choices"][0]["message"]["content"] = 5
    if shape == "surrogate":
        s = json.dumps(b).replace("Le jardin", "\\ud800 Le jardin", 1)
        return s.encode()
    if shape == "non_utf8": return b'{"model": "x", "content": "\xff\xfe\xfa"}'
    if shape == "empty_body": return b""
    if shape == "content_parts_bad": b["choices"] = [{"message": {"content": [1, 2]}, "finish_reason": "stop"}] if route != "anthropic" else b["choices"] if "choices" in b else None
    return json.dumps(b).encode()

class H(BaseHTTPRequestHandler):
    def log_message(self, *a): pass
    def do_POST(self):
        body = json.loads(self.rfile.read(int(self.headers["Content-Length"])).decode())
        route = self.path.strip("/"); model = body["model"]
        if route == "openai" and "temperature" in body:
            msg = b'{"error": {"message": "Unsupported parameter: temperature is not supported with this model."}}'
            self.send_response(400); self.send_header("Content-Length", str(len(msg))); self.end_headers(); self.wfile.write(msg); return
        with LOCK:
            N[route] = N.get(route, 0) + 1; k = N[route]
        data = weird(route, model, SHAPE) if (k == K and SHAPE != "none") else json.dumps(normal(route, model)).encode()
        self.send_response(200); self.send_header("Content-Type", "application/json"); self.send_header("Content-Length", str(len(data))); self.end_headers(); self.wfile.write(data)
    def do_GET(self):
        data = b'{"data": []}'; self.send_response(200); self.send_header("Content-Length", str(len(data))); self.end_headers(); self.wfile.write(data)

if __name__ == "__main__":
    ThreadingHTTPServer(("127.0.0.1", int(sys.argv[1])), H).serve_forever()

    for f in sorted(glob.glob(w + "/data/raw/*.jsonl")):

# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
"""N1 budget runs: pipeline.run_calls(...) N times in a row against a mock provider, with a plan budget.
After each run: exit status, tracebacks, money counted from the raw records (what pipeline.spent_on counts), money the mock
provider would have billed (its own log), the budget.
usage: n1_run.py LABEL CODE CAP NRUNS MOCK [--restart-mock] [--cut-prob P] [--cut-orig 0|1] [--fast] [--delay S]

import glob
import json
import os
import shutil
import socket
import subprocess
import sys
import time

def free_port():
    s = socket.socket(); s.bind(("127.0.0.1", 0)); p = s.getsockname()[1]; s.close(); return p


def setup(label, code):
    w = f"{ROOT}/v041_check/runs/{label}"
    shutil.rmtree(w, ignore_errors=True)
    os.makedirs(w + "/data/lexicon")
    for f in glob.glob(CODE[code] + "/*.py") + [CODE[code] + "/design.json", CODE[code] + "/calls_pilot.jsonl"]:
        shutil.copy(f, w)
    os.symlink(LEX, w + "/data/lexicon/Lexique383.tsv")
    json.dump(MODELS, open(w + "/models.json", "w"))
    return w


def start_mock(mock, w, args, tag):
    port = free_port()
    env = dict(os.environ, MOCK_LOG=f"{w}/mock_{tag}.jsonl", CUT_PROB=str(args["cut_prob"]), CUT_ORIG=str(args["cut_orig"]),
               DELAY=str(args["delay"]))
    p = subprocess.Popen([sys.executable, "-B", MOCKS[mock], str(port)], env=env, stdout=open(f"{w}/mock_{tag}.log", "w"), stderr=subprocess.STDOUT)
    time.sleep(1.0)
    return p, port


def counted(w, prefix="pilot-"):
    tot = rep = poss = 0.0; n = ok = 0
    for f in sorted(glob.glob(w + "/data/raw/*.jsonl")):
        for l in open(f, encoding="utf-8"):
            r = json.loads(l)
            if str(r.get("call_id", "")).startswith(prefix):
                n += 1; ok += bool(r.get("ok"))
                rep += float(r.get("cost_usd", 0.0)); poss += float(r.get("cost_usd_possibly_charged", 0.0))
    return rep + poss, rep, poss, n, ok


def billed(w):
    tot = 0.0; ev = {}
    for f in glob.glob(w + "/mock_*.jsonl"):
        for l in open(f, encoding="utf-8"):
            r = json.loads(l)
            pin, pout = PRICES[r["route"]]
            tot += (r["in"] * pin + r["out"] * pout) / 1e6
            ev[r["event"]] = ev.get(r["event"], 0) + 1
    return tot, ev


def main():
    a = sys.argv[1:]
    label, code, cap, nruns, mock = a[0], a[1], float(a[2]), int(a[3]), a[4]
    opts = {"cut_prob": 0.0, "cut_orig": 1, "delay": 0.0}
    fast = "--fast" in a
    restart = "--restart-mock" in a
    for i, x in enumerate(a):
        if x == "--cut-prob": opts["cut_prob"] = float(a[i + 1])
        if x == "--cut-orig": opts["cut_orig"] = int(a[i + 1])
        if x == "--delay": opts["delay"] = float(a[i + 1])
    w = setup(label, code)
    print(f"== {label}: code {code}, plan budget {cap} USD, {nruns} runs, mock {mock}, restart_mock={restart}, {opts}, fast={fast}")
    mp = None
    worst_over = 0.0
    for k in range(1, nruns + 1):
        if mp is None or restart:
            if mp: mp.terminate(); mp.wait()
            mp, port = start_mock(mock, w, opts, f"run{k}")
        env = dict(os.environ, ART5_ENDPOINT_OVERRIDE=f"http://127.0.0.1:{port}", ANTHROPIC_API_KEY="x", OPENAI_API_KEY="x",
                   MISTRAL_API_KEY="x", PYTHONDONTWRITEBYTECODE="1")
        if fast:
            env["PYTHONPATH"] = ROOT + "/v041_check/scripts/fast"
        t0 = time.time()
        r = subprocess.run([sys.executable, "-B", "-c", f"import pipeline; pipeline.run_calls('calls_pilot.jsonl', {cap}, 10.0)"],
                           cwd=w, env=env, capture_output=True, text=True)
        out = r.stdout + r.stderr
        open(f"{w}/run{k}.out", "w").write(out)
        tot, rep, poss, n, ok = counted(w)
        b, ev = billed(w)
        tb = out.count("Traceback")
        logs = len(glob.glob(w + "/logs/run_*.json"))
        over = tot - cap
        worst_over = max(worst_over, over)
        print(f"  run {k}: exit {r.returncode}, tracebacks {tb}, {time.time() - t0:.0f}s | raw records {n} (ok {ok}), run logs {logs} | "
              f"counted from raw {tot:.4f} (reported {rep:.4f} + possibly charged {poss:.4f}) vs budget {cap} -> {'OVER by %.4f' % over if over > 1e-9 else 'within'} | "
              f"mock would have billed {b:.4f} {ev}")
        for l in out.splitlines():
            if "spent_usd_total" in l or "not run" in l or "stopped with an error" in l or "Traceback" in l:
                print("      ", l[:200])
    if mp: mp.terminate(); mp.wait()
    print(f"  workdir {w}")


main()

    return "kept+FLAG" if r["flag"] else "kept"
