# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
"""N3 (lines without letters) and N5 (Voici, Note, closing lines): cases written for this check, v0.4 against v0.4.1."""
import itertools
import re
import sys
sys.path.insert(0, "/tmp/claude-0/-home-claude/d28672eb-31f4-533e-85fc-00a96d822022/scratchpad/v041_check/scripts")
from lib41 import load, verdict

new, lex = load("v041")
old, _ = load("v04")
S1 = "Un petit lapin sortit de son terrier. Il regarda le ciel bleu et les nuages blancs."
S2 = "Puis il courut dans le pré pour rejoindre ses amis. Ils jouèrent jusqu'au soir."
S3 = "Quand la nuit tomba, le lapin rentra chez lui et s'endormit."


def canon(s):
    s = new.normalise(s)
    s = re.sub(r"^(titre|title)\s*:\s*", "", re.sub(r"[#*_]+", " ", s).strip(), flags=re.I)
    return re.sub(r"[\W_]+", "", s.lower())


# ------------------------------------------------------------------ N3: separators at every slot
SEPS = ["---", "***", "___", "* * *", "- - -", "—————", "=====", "~~~~", ". . .", "• • •", "—"]
PRE = "Voici une histoire pour des élèves de 8 à 9 ans :"
TITLE = "# Le lapin curieux"
BODY = [S1, S2, S3]
POST = ["Bonne lecture !", "N'hésitez pas à me demander une autre version."]


def build(slots):
    """slots: dict slot index -> separator. 0 before preface, 1 preface/title, 2 title/body, 3 between body 1 and 2,
    4 body/closings, 5 between closings, 6 after the last closing."""
    out = []
    def sep(i):
        if i in slots:
            out.append(slots[i])
    sep(0); out.append(PRE); sep(1); out.append(TITLE); sep(2); out.append(BODY[0]); sep(3); out += BODY[1:]
    sep(4); out.append(POST[0]); sep(5); out.append(POST[1]); sep(6)
    return "\n\n".join(out)


def check_n3(mod):
    bad, n = [], 0
    combos = [dict()] + [{i: s} for i in range(7) for s in SEPS] + \
             [{i: s1, j: s2} for i, j in itertools.combinations(range(7), 2) for s1 in SEPS[:5] for s2 in SEPS[:5]]
    for slots in combos:
        text = build(slots)
        info = mod.clean_info(text)
        got = [canon(l) for l in info["lines"]]
        ok = (canon(info["title"]) == canon(TITLE) and got == [canon(b) for b in BODY] and info["n_meta_lines"] == 3)
        n += 1
        if not ok:
            bad.append((slots, info["title"], info["n_meta_lines"], len(got)))
    return n, bad


for name, mod in (("v0.4", old), ("v0.4.1", new)):
    n, bad = check_n3(mod)
    print(f"N3 {name}: {n} layouts with 11 kinds of separator line at 7 positions (one or two per answer): {len(bad)} wrong"
          + (f"; first: {bad[0]}" if bad else ""))

# a separator line that carries a letter must stay a line (not dropped): "--- fin ---" is text
t = "Le lapin curieux\n\n" + S1 + "\n\n--- fin ---\n\n" + S2
print("N3 control, '--- fin ---' keeps its letters:", new.clean_info(t)["lines"])
# an answer made only of separators
print("N3 control, answer '---' only:", verdict(new, lex, "---")["excl"], "| '---\\n---':", verdict(new, lex, "---\n---")["excl"])
# digits-only line (page number, list marker alone) is also dropped; check it does not change the measure
t_digits = "Le lapin curieux\n\n" + S1 + "\n\n1\n\n" + S2 + "\n\n2.\n\n" + S3
a, b = verdict(old, lex, t_digits), verdict(new, lex, t_digits)
print("N3 control, digit-only lines: words v0.4 %d v0.4.1 %d, mean %.4f / %.4f" % (a["words"], b["words"], a["mean"], b["mean"]))

# ------------------------------------------------------------------ N5: Voici
print("\nN5 'Voici' first lines (answer without title line, 2 body paragraphs); 'removed' = first paragraph dropped as preface")
REST = "\n\n" + S2 + " " + S3
VOICI = [
    ("genuine", "Voici Tom, un garçon de CM1 qui rêvait de voler. Chaque matin, il regardait les oiseaux dans la cour de l'école."),
    ("genuine", "Voici la maison de mamie Rose. Elle est petite et jaune, avec des volets verts et un jardin plein de fleurs."),
    ("genuine", "Voici l'histoire de Mila, une petite fille de huit ans qui adorait les volcans et leurs secrets."),
    ("genuine", "Voici Noah, qui est en 5H cette année. Il adore dessiner des volcans sur tous ses cahiers."),
    ("genuine", "Voici un petit village au bord de la mer. Ses maisons blanches brillent au soleil."),
    ("genuine", "Voici l'histoire de Mila, une princesse à la destinée étrange. Elle gardait le château avec son chien."),
    ("genuine", "Voici le village de Saint-Luc, au niveau de la rivière. Les maisons y sont bâties sur des pilotis."),
    ("genuine", "Voici Léa, une élève qui n'était pas adaptée au bruit de la cantine. Elle préférait la bibliothèque."),
    ("preface", "Voici une histoire sur les volcans."),
    ("preface", "Voici un texte documentaire sur les pompiers."),
    ("preface", "Voici une courte histoire pour tes élèves."),
    ("preface", "Voici une histoire pour des élèves de 5H."),
    ("preface", "Voici une version adaptée aux élèves de 5H."),
    ("preface", "Voici un petit texte sur les volcans."),
    ("preface", "Voici ton histoire sur les volcans."),
    ("preface", "Voici le texte demandé."),
]
cnt = {"genuine": [0, 0], "preface": [0, 0]}
for kind, first in VOICI:
    text = first + REST
    a, b = new.clean_info(text), old.clean_info(text)
    removed_new = canon(first) not in [canon(l) for l in a["lines"]] and canon(first) != canon(a["title"])
    removed_old = canon(first) not in [canon(l) for l in b["lines"]] and canon(first) != canon(b["title"])
    good_new = (removed_new == (kind == "preface"))
    good_old = (removed_old == (kind == "preface"))
    cnt[kind][0] += good_old
    cnt[kind][1] += good_new
    print(f"  {kind:8s} v0.4 {'removed' if removed_old else 'kept   '} {'ok' if good_old else 'XX'} | v0.4.1 {'removed' if removed_new else 'kept   '} {'ok' if good_new else 'XX'} | {first[:70]}")
print("  as expected: genuine kept v0.4 %d/8 v0.4.1 %d/8 | prefaces removed v0.4 %d/8 v0.4.1 %d/8" % (cnt["genuine"][0], cnt["genuine"][1], cnt["preface"][0], cnt["preface"][1]))

# ------------------------------------------------------------------ N5: closing Note lines

    ("subject", "Note : la lave peut atteindre 1 000 degrés."),
    ("subject", "Remarque : le Vésuve est un volcan italien célèbre."),
    ("subject", "Note : le niveau de la mer peut monter quand les glaciers fondent."),
    ("subject", "Note : l'histoire du Vésuve commence il y a des milliers d'années."),
    ("subject", "Note : le mot volcan vient de Vulcain, le dieu romain du feu."),
    ("subject", "Note : les mots « lave » et « magma » ne désignent pas la même chose."),
    ("subject", "Remarque : les phrases « le volcan dort » et « le volcan est éteint » ne veulent pas dire la même chose."),
    ("subject", "Note : la lecture d'un sismographe aide à prévoir les éruptions."),
    ("subject", "Note : les élèves de l'école de Pompéi ne sont jamais revenus chez eux."),
    ("subject", "Note bien ce que tu vois, dit le guide."),
    ("subject", "Note bien les mots du guide, dit Léa."),
]
ok = {"text": [0, 0], "subject": [0, 0]}
tot = {"text": 0, "subject": 0}
for kind, note in NOTES:
    text = BASE + note
    a, b = new.clean_info(text), old.clean_info(text)
    r_new = canon(note) not in [canon(l) for l in a["lines"]]
    r_old = canon(note) not in [canon(l) for l in b["lines"]]
    want = kind == "text"
    tot[kind] += 1
    ok[kind][0] += (r_old == want)
    ok[kind][1] += (r_new == want)
    print(f"  {kind:7s} v0.4 {'removed' if r_old else 'kept   '} {'ok' if r_old == want else 'XX'} | v0.4.1 {'removed' if r_new else 'kept   '} {'ok' if r_new == want else 'XX'} | {note[:80]}")
print("  notes about the text removed: v0.4 %d/%d, v0.4.1 %d/%d | notes about the subject kept: v0.4 %d/%d, v0.4.1 %d/%d" % (
    ok["text"][0], tot["text"], ok["text"][1], tot["text"], ok["subject"][0], tot["subject"], ok["subject"][1], tot["subject"]))

# ------------------------------------------------------------------ N5: word counts and Dites-moi
print("\nN5 other closing lines (expected: removed)")
CLOSE = ["(Environ 80 mots)", "(80 mots)", "Environ 80 mots.", "80 mots", "(≈ 80 mots)", "(80 mots environ)", "(Nombre de mots : 80)",
         "Longueur : environ 80 mots", "*(environ 80 mots)*", "[80 mots]", "Dites-moi si vous voulez une suite !", "Dis-moi si tu veux une autre histoire !",
         "Dis-moi, quel est ton animal préféré ?", "Dites-moi ce que vous en pensez."]
for c in CLOSE:
    text = BASE + c
    a, b = new.clean_info(text), old.clean_info(text)
    r_new = canon(c) not in [canon(l) for l in a["lines"]]
    r_old = canon(c) not in [canon(l) for l in b["lines"]]
    print(f"  v0.4 {'removed' if r_old else 'kept   '} | v0.4.1 {'removed' if r_new else 'kept   '} | flag_meta v0.4.1 {verdict(new, lex, text)['flag']!s:5s} | {c}")

===== FILE readers/v041_check/scripts/t_new10.py | 3618 bytes | sha256 acafd81591d81d7138cdd194ab33068f0d7aaa9718077a4c6894313677f4104d =====
# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
"""Run the 10 new answers (answers41.py) through the cleaning, refusal and flag rules of v0.4 and v0.4.1.
Same checks as the v0.4 battery (t6_battery.py): genuine line removed, meta line left in, wrong title, excluded, flagged."""
import json
import re
import sys
sys.path.insert(0, "/tmp/claude-0/-home-claude/d28672eb-31f4-533e-85fc-00a96d822022/scratchpad/v041_check/scripts")
from lib41 import load
from answers41 import A

LIST_RE = re.compile(r"^(- |\d+\. )")


def render(pieces):
    out, prev = [], None
    for kind, txt in pieces:
        if kind == "title" and txt == "x":
            continue
        if out and prev is not None and LIST_RE.match(txt) and LIST_RE.match(prev):
            out[-1] += "\n" + txt
        else:
            out.append(txt)
        prev = txt
    return "\n\n".join(out)


def run(which):
    mod, lex = load(which)

    def canon(s):
        s = mod.normalise(s)
        s = re.sub(r"^(titre|title)\s*:\s*", "", re.sub(r"[#*_]+", " ", s).strip(), flags=re.I)
        return re.sub(r"[\W_]+", "", s.lower())

    rows = []
    for a in A:
        text = render(a["pieces"])
        info = mod.clean_info(text)
        out_lines = [canon(l) for l in info["lines"]]
        out_title = canon(info["title"])
        exp_title = next((canon(t) for k, t in a["pieces"] if k == "title" and t != "x"), "")
        bodies = [(canon(t), t) for k, t in a["pieces"] if k == "body"]
        metas = [(canon(t), t) for k, t in a["pieces"] if k == "meta"]
        lost = [t for c, t in bodies if c not in out_lines and c != out_title]
        leaked = [t for c, t in metas if c in out_lines]
        m, _ = mod.measure(info["lines"], lex)
        rec = {"ok": True, "finish_reason": "stop", "text": text}
        reason = mod.exclusion_reason(rec, m)
        fm = mod.flag_meta(text)
        rows.append(dict(id=a["id"], plaus=a["plaus"], words=m["n_words"], n_meta=info["n_meta_lines"], exp_meta=len(metas),
                         title_ok=(out_title == exp_title), lost=lost, leaked=leaked, excl=reason, flag=fm,
                         mean=m["mean_zipf_content"], note=a["note"]))
    return rows


old, new = run("v04"), run("v041")
for r0, r1 in zip(old, new):
    def line(r):
        issue = bool(r["lost"] or r["leaked"] or not r["title_ok"] or r["excl"] or (r["flag"] and r["exp_meta"] == 0))
        return (f"words={r['words']:3d} meta {r['n_meta']}/{r['exp_meta']} title_ok={str(r['title_ok']):5s} "
                f"excl={r['excl'] or '-':6s} flag={'Y' if r['flag'] else '-'} mean={r['mean']:.3f} {'<<< ISSUE' if issue else ''}")
    print(f"{r1['id']} {r1['plaus'][:5]} | v0.4  {line(r0)}")
    print(f"{'':10s} | v0.4.1 {line(r1)}")
    for tag, r in (("v0.4", r0), ("v0.4.1", r1)):
        if r["lost"]:
            print(f"{'':10s}   {tag}: genuine line REMOVED:", [x[:60] for x in r["lost"]])
        if r["leaked"]:
            print(f"{'':10s}   {tag}: meta line LEFT IN:", [x[:60] for x in r["leaked"]])
        if r["flag"] and r["exp_meta"] == 0:
            print(f"{'':10s}   {tag}: genuine text FLAGGED")
json.dump({"v04": old, "v041": new}, open("new10_results.json", "w"), ensure_ascii=False, indent=1)


def summ(rows, label):
    print(f"{label}: n={len(rows)} | genuine line removed in {sum(bool(r['lost']) for r in rows)} | meta left in {sum(bool(r['leaked']) for r in rows)} | "
          f"title wrong {sum(not r['title_ok'] for r in rows)} | excluded {sum(bool(r['excl']) for r in rows)} | "
          f"genuine flagged {sum(r['flag'] and r['exp_meta'] == 0 for r in rows)}")


print()
summ(old, "v0.4  ")
summ(new, "v0.4.1")

# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
"""Hand-written answers of the kind current chat models give to
'Écris {une histoire à lire | un texte documentaire à lire} pour des élèves {label}, sur le thème {topic}.
 Réponds seulement avec un titre et le texte.'
Each answer is a list of pieces (kind, text). kinds:
  title  = the title line (rendered as given, may carry markdown)
  body   = a genuine text line that must survive cleaning and be measured
  meta   = a preface/closing line addressed to the user, that should be removed (R2b)
  label  = a label line ('Titre :', 'Texte :') that should disappear
  rule   = a markdown horizontal rule or other non-text decoration (ignored by the check)
'plausible' = what I would expect a current chat model to produce at a non-negligible rate (my judgement, inference);
'edge'      = constructed to probe a rule, would be rare in practice (inference).
Pieces are joined by a blank line, except consecutive 'body' pieces flagged 'tight' which are joined by a newline.
"""
A = []
def add(id, kind, plaus, genre, pieces, note=""):
    A.append(dict(id=id, kind=kind, plaus=plaus, genre=genre, pieces=pieces, note=note))

===== FILE readers/v04_check/scripts/common.py | 914 bytes | sha256 a5d2519b4b8f1c22e06bf66062a34ecdf1d1e941653c7c5ce1681c9ea38d213d =====
"""Shared loader for the v0.4 check: imports compute.py from the check copy (never edits it) and caches the lexicon."""
import os, pickle, sys, importlib
ART5 = "/tmp/claude-0/-home-claude/d28672eb-31f4-533e-85fc-00a96d822022/scratchpad/v04_check/art5"
LEXPATH = "/usr/local/lib/python3.13/site-packages/pylexique/Lexique383/Lexique383.txt"
CACHE = "/tmp/claude-0/-home-claude/d28672eb-31f4-533e-85fc-00a96d822022/scratchpad/v04_check/scripts/lex_v04.pkl"

def load(art5=None):
    art5 = art5 or os.environ.get("ART5_DIR", ART5)
    sys.path.insert(0, art5)
    for m in ("compute",):
        sys.modules.pop(m, None)
    import compute
    importlib.reload(compute)
    if art5 == ART5 and os.path.exists(CACHE):
        lex = pickle.load(open(CACHE, "rb"))
    else:
        lex, _ = compute.load_lexicon(LEXPATH)
        if art5 == ART5:
            pickle.dump(lex, open(CACHE, "wb"))
    return compute, lex

===== FILE readers/v04_check/scripts/d5_driver.py | 1160 bytes | sha256 53f1c5df21a3e0b3a18166553f6ea72d698b0f7ee44d81fbd7df1718b7bc404a =====
"""Drive pipeline.run_calls(...) in a working folder against the mock; print the money spent as counted three ways."""
import os, sys, json, glob
W, calls, cap = sys.argv[1], sys.argv[2], float(sys.argv[3])
os.chdir(W); sys.path.insert(0, W)
import pipeline
def raw():
    tot = n = ok = 0
    for f in sorted(glob.glob("data/raw/*.jsonl")):
        for l in open(f, encoding="utf-8"):
            r = json.loads(l); tot += r.get("cost_usd", 0.0); n += 1; ok += bool(r.get("ok"))
    return round(tot, 6), n, ok
def logs():
    tot = 0.0; runs = []
    for f in sorted(glob.glob("logs/run_*.json")):
        r = json.load(open(f)); tot += r["spent_usd"]; runs.append((os.path.basename(r["calls_file"]), r["spent_usd"], r["cap_usd"], r["stopped"]))
    return round(tot, 6), runs
print(f"--- before: raw cost/records/ok {raw()}  logged total {logs()[0]}  runs so far {len(logs()[1])}")
pipeline.run_calls(calls, cap, 10.0)
r = raw(); l = logs()
print(f"--- after : raw cost/records/ok {r}  logged total {l[0]}  cap {cap}")
for x in l[1]: print("      run", x)
print("RESULT raw_cost <= cap:", r[0] <= cap + 1e-12, "| logged_total <= cap:", l[0] <= cap + 1e-12)

===== FILE readers/v04_check/scripts/mock_bad.py | 4495 bytes | sha256 2a77b57f0d62117eb6637adbb5c1bea86b300dc9eea24ec196d43ad5d5e411cf =====
"""Offline stand-in for the three provider APIs, for the end-to-end test of the ART 5 pipeline only.
Texts are generated from a small word list; easier words for younger requested ages. Not data."""
import json, random, re, sys, threading
from http.server import ThreadingHTTPServer, BaseHTTPRequestHandler

EASY = "chat chien maison jardin soleil pain eau arbre fleur école ami jouer manger courir voir petit grand beau content".split()
HARD = "insecte pollen colonie nectar ruche éruption magma cratère migration altitude hémisphère photosynthèse écosystème".split()
COUNT = {"n": 0}
SEEN = set()
LOCK = threading.Lock()

def age_of(prompt):
    m = re.search(r"de (\d+) à (\d+) ans", prompt)
    if m:
        return (int(m.group(1)) + int(m.group(2))) / 2
    m = re.search(r"de (\d)[HP]\b", prompt)
    if m:
        return int(m.group(1)) + 3.5
    return 9.0

def make_text(prompt, rng):
    if prompt.startswith("À quel âge") or "quel âge" in prompt:
        g = re.search(r"(\d)[HP]", prompt)
        n = int(g.group(1)) if g else 5
        return f"Les élèves de {n}H ont en général entre {n + 3} et {n + 4} ans.", "stop"
    a = age_of(prompt)
    p_hard = min(max((a - 5) / 12, 0.05), 0.6)
    n = rng.randint(60, 160)
    words = [rng.choice(HARD) if rng.random() < p_hard else rng.choice(EASY) for _ in range(n)]
    sents, i = [], 0
    while i < len(words):
        k = rng.randint(6, 12)
        s = " ".join(words[i:i + k])
        sents.append("Le " + s + ".")
        i += k
    return "Le jardin des abeilles\n\n" + " ".join(sents), "stop"

class H(BaseHTTPRequestHandler):
    def log_message(self, *a):
        pass
    def do_POST(self):
        body = json.loads(self.rfile.read(int(self.headers["Content-Length"])).decode())
        prompt = body["messages"][0]["content"]
        if self.path.strip("/") == "openai" and "temperature" in body:  # this mock model refuses the parameter
            msg = b'{"error": {"message": "Unsupported parameter: temperature is not supported with this model."}}'
            self.send_response(400); self.send_header("Content-Length", str(len(msg))); self.end_headers(); self.wfile.write(msg); return
        rng = random.Random(prompt + body["model"])
        with LOCK:
            COUNT["n"] += 1
            n = COUNT["n"]
        if n in (5, 30):  # connection drops in the middle of a 200 response body (IncompleteRead on the client)
            self.send_response(200); self.send_header("Content-Length", "5000"); self.end_headers()
            self.wfile.write(b'{"model": "x", "choices": ['); self.wfile.flush(); self.close_connection = True; return
        if n == 3:  # one transient rate-limit error
            self.send_response(429); self.end_headers(); self.wfile.write(b'{"error":"rate"}'); return
        text, finish = make_text(prompt, rng)
        key = prompt + body["model"]
        with LOCK:
            first = key not in SEEN
            SEEN.add(key)
        if first and rng.random() < 0.06:
            text, finish = "Voici un texte court.", "stop"  # too short on the first request only
        if first and rng.random() < 0.04:  # a transport failure on the first request only
            msg = b'{"error": "bad gateway at the mock"}'
            self.send_response(400); self.send_header("Content-Length", str(len(msg))); self.end_headers(); self.wfile.write(msg); return
        if rng.random() < 0.02:
            finish = "length"
        provider = self.path.strip("/")
        if provider == "anthropic":
            out = {"model": body["model"] + "-20261001", "content": [{"type": "text", "text": text}],
                   "stop_reason": "end_turn" if finish == "stop" else "max_tokens",
                   "usage": {"input_tokens": 40, "output_tokens": 3800}}
        else:
            out = {"model": body["model"], "choices": [{"message": {"content": text}, "finish_reason": finish}],
                   "usage": {"prompt_tokens": 40, "completion_tokens": 3800}}
        data = json.dumps(out).encode()
        self.send_response(200); self.send_header("Content-Type", "application/json"); self.send_header("Content-Length", str(len(data))); self.end_headers(); self.wfile.write(data)
    def do_GET(self):
        data = b'{"data": []}'
        self.send_response(200); self.send_header("Content-Length", str(len(data))); self.end_headers(); self.wfile.write(data)

if __name__ == "__main__":
    ThreadingHTTPServer(("127.0.0.1", int(sys.argv[1])), H).serve_forever()

            self.send_response(429); self.end_headers(); self.wfile.write(b'{"error":"rate"}'); return
        text, finish = make_text(prompt, rng)
        key = prompt + body["model"]
        with LOCK:
            first = key not in SEEN
            SEEN.add(key)
        if first and rng.random() < 0.06:
            text, finish = "Voici un texte court.", "stop"  # too short on the first request only
        if first and rng.random() < 0.04:  # a transport failure on the first request only
            msg = b'{"error": "bad gateway at the mock"}'
            self.send_response(400); self.send_header("Content-Length", str(len(msg))); self.end_headers(); self.wfile.write(msg); return
        if rng.random() < 0.02:
            finish = "length"
        provider = self.path.strip("/")
        if provider == "anthropic":
            out = {"model": body["model"] + "-20261001", "content": [{"type": "text", "text": text}],
                   "stop_reason": "end_turn" if finish == "stop" else "max_tokens",
                   "usage": {"input_tokens": 40, "output_tokens": 3800}}
        else:
            out = {"model": body["model"], "choices": [{"message": {"content": text}, "finish_reason": finish}],
                   "usage": {"prompt_tokens": 40, "completion_tokens": 3800}}
        data = json.dumps(out).encode()
        self.send_response(200); self.send_header("Content-Type", "application/json"); self.send_header("Content-Length", str(len(data))); self.end_headers(); self.wfile.write(data)
    def do_GET(self):
        data = b'{"data": []}'
        self.send_response(200); self.send_header("Content-Length", str(len(data))); self.end_headers(); self.wfile.write(data)

if __name__ == "__main__":
    ThreadingHTTPServer(("127.0.0.1", int(sys.argv[1])), H).serve_forever()

===== FILE readers/v04_check/scripts/t11_factorial.py | 4807 bytes | sha256 df4e4e1d1ef53feaeee1fffa5a217aefd59a26928aaea12a2a997446d55a3fea =====
# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
"""Full factorial of mainstream answer shapes: does cleaning keep every body line, find the title, remove exactly the meta lines?"""
import sys, re, itertools, collections, random
sys.path.insert(0, "/tmp/claude-0/-home-claude/d28672eb-31f4-533e-85fc-00a96d822022/scratchpad/v04_check/scripts")
from common import load
compute, lex = load()
def canon(s):
    s = compute.normalise(s)
    s = re.sub(r"^(titre|title)\s*:\s*", "", re.sub(r"[#*_]+", " ", s).strip(), flags=re.I)
    return re.sub(r"[\W_]+", "", s.lower())

PRE = {"none": [], "bien_sur_voici_colon": ["Bien sûr ! Voici une histoire pour des élèves de 5H :"],
       "voici_audience_nocolon": ["Voici un texte pour des élèves de 8 à 9 ans."], "voici_votre_colon": ["Voici votre texte :"]}
RULE = {"none": [], "rule": ["---"]}
TITLE = {"h1": "# Le grand volcan", "h2": "## Le grand volcan", "bold": "**Le grand volcan**", "plain": "Le grand volcan",
         "label": "Titre : Le grand volcan", "boldlabel": "**Titre : Le grand volcan**", "guillemets": "« Le grand volcan »", "question": "Qui réveille le volcan ?"}
LABEL = {"none": [], "bold_texte": ["**Texte :**"], "plain_texte": ["Texte :"], "bold_histoire": ["**Histoire**"], "plain_histoire": ["Histoire :"]}
BODY = {
 "story_dash": ["Au pied d'une haute montagne vivait une petite fille nommée Léa. Chaque matin, elle regardait la fumée blanche qui montait du volcan.",
                "— Pourquoi fume-t-il ? demanda Léa à son grand-père.", "— Il respire, répondit le vieil homme. Il dort, mais il respire.",
                "Ce soir-là, Léa s'endormit en pensant au géant de pierre."],
 "story_guillemets": ["Il était une fois un volcan qui avait très faim. Il grondait et il toussait.", "« J'ai faim ! » cria-t-il.",
                      "Les oiseaux apportèrent des graines et des feuilles. Le volcan les avala et sourit.", "« Merci, mes amis », dit-il enfin."],
 "doc_bullets": ["Un volcan est une montagne qui crache du feu. Sous la terre, la roche est très chaude.", "## Les parties d'un volcan",
                 "- **Le cratère** : c'est l'ouverture en haut.", "- **La cheminée** : le magma monte par ce tunnel.",
                 "**Le savais-tu ?** Certains volcans se trouvent au fond de la mer."],
}
POST = {"none": [], "fin": ["Fin"], "moral": ["Morale : il ne faut jamais réveiller un volcan qui dort."],
        "tu_question": ["Et toi, aimerais-tu visiter un volcan ?"], "tu_closing": ["Alors, la prochaine fois que tu verras une montagne qui fume, pense au géant de pierre."],
        "meta_hesitez": ["N'hésitez pas à me demander une autre version !"], "meta_bonne_lecture": ["Bonne lecture !"],
        "rule_meta": ["---", "J'espère que cette histoire plaira à vos élèves."], "meta_rule": ["J'espère que cette histoire plaira à vos élèves.", "---"]}
META_POST = {"meta_hesitez", "meta_bonne_lecture", "rule_meta", "meta_rule"}

fails = collections.Counter(); n = 0; examples = {}
for pre, rule1, tit, lab, rule2, body, post in itertools.product(PRE, RULE, TITLE, LABEL, RULE, BODY, POST):
    if pre == "none" and rule1 == "rule":   # a rule before the title with no preface is not a realistic shape
        continue
    lines = list(PRE[pre]) + RULE[rule1] + [TITLE[tit]] + LABEL[lab] + RULE[rule2] + BODY[body] + POST[post]
    # keep tight bullet lists on single newlines
    text = "\n\n".join(lines)
    info = compute.clean_info(text)
    got_lines = [canon(l) for l in info["lines"]]
    body_exp = [canon(b) for b in BODY[body]] + ([canon(POST[post][0])] if post in ("fin", "moral", "tu_question", "tu_closing") else [])
    exp_meta = len(PRE[pre]) + (1 if post in META_POST else 0)
    lost = [b for b in body_exp if b not in got_lines]
    title_ok = canon(info["title"]) == canon(TITLE[tit])
    leaked_meta = any(canon(m) in got_lines for m in PRE[pre] + (POST[post] if post in META_POST else []) if canon(m))
    refusal = compute.is_refusal_text(text)
    key = (pre, rule1, tit, lab, rule2, body, post)
    n += 1
    bad = []
    if lost: bad.append("lost_body")
    if not title_ok: bad.append("title")
    if info["n_meta_lines"] != exp_meta: bad.append("meta_count")
    if refusal: bad.append("refusal")
    if bad:
        for b in bad: fails[b] += 1
        sig = (b := ",".join(bad), pre, rule1, tit if "title" in bad else "*", lab if "title" in bad else "*", rule2 if "title" in bad else "*", post if "meta_count" in bad or "lost_body" in bad else "*")
        examples.setdefault(sig, (text, info["title"], info["n_meta_lines"], exp_meta))
print(f"{n} combinations;", dict(fails))
for sig, (text, t, got, exp) in sorted(examples.items())[:14]:
    print("\nSIG", sig, "| title found", repr(t), "| meta", got, "/", exp)
    print("   ", text[:260].replace("\n", " ⏎ "))

"""D1: refusal detection. Cases NOT in compute.py's REFUSALS / KEPT_DESPITE_PHRASE."""
import sys

    rec = {"ok": True, "finish_reason": "stop", "text": text}
    m = compute.measure(compute.clean(text)[1], lex)[0]
    return compute.exclusion_reason(rec, m)

 ("vous hook: Pouvez-vous expliquer (documentary)", "Les volcans\nPouvez-vous expliquer pourquoi un volcan crache du feu ? Voici la réponse. " + STORY),

 ("Désolé at para start with le", "Le lapin\nDésolé, le lapin n'avait pas fait exprès. " + STORY),
]

def run(name, cases, expect):
    bad = []
    print(f"== {name}")
    for lab, text in cases:
        r = reason(text)
        got = "refusal" if r == "refusal" else ("kept" if r == "" else r)
        fm = compute.flag_meta(text)
        flag = "OK " if got == expect else "XX "
        print(f"  {flag}{lab[:62]:62s} -> {got:14s} flag_meta={fm}")
        if got != expect:
            bad.append(lab)
    return bad

b1 = run("must be excluded as refusal/clarification (beyond self-test)", MUST_EXCLUDE, "refusal")
b2 = run("known-limit: clarifications not starting with a listed phrase (expect refusal; failures = missed)", KNOWN_LIMIT, "refusal")
b3 = run("genuine texts that must be kept", MUST_KEEP, "kept")
print("\nSUMMARY: missed in MUST_EXCLUDE:", b1)
print("missed in KNOWN_LIMIT (n=%d/%d):" % (len(b2), len(KNOWN_LIMIT)), b2)
print("wrongly excluded genuine texts:", b3)

"""D2: ligatures. (a) real Latin-1 lexicon (spells oe), (b) a UTF-8 copy of the real lexicon re-spelled with the ligature
(as the official Lexique383.tsv is, inference), (c) tiny lexicons spelled cœur / œil / Œ-capital, (d) v0.3-style loader (no folding)."""
import sys, re, tempfile, os, importlib
sys.path.insert(0, "/tmp/claude-0/-home-claude/d28672eb-31f4-533e-85fc-00a96d822022/scratchpad/v04_check/scripts")
from common import load, LEXPATH
import pandas as pd
compute, lex = load()

TEXT = "Ma sœur a mal à l'œil. Le bœuf mange un œuf. Mon cœur bat. Le nœud est serré. Elle fait un vœu. Cette œuvre est belle. Le chœur chante."
def stats(lx, text=TEXT):
    m, lem = compute.measure(compute.clean(text)[1], lx)
    return round(m["share_oov"], 3), m["n_content"], round(m["mean_zipf_content"], 4), lem

print("(a) real Latin-1 lexicon:", stats(lex))

# (b) build a UTF-8 copy of the real lexicon where the usual oe-ligature words are spelled with the ligature
LIG = ["coeur", "soeur", "oeil", "oeuf", "boeuf", "noeud", "voeu", "oeuvre", "choeur", "moeurs", "manoeuvre"]
df = pd.read_csv(LEXPATH, sep="\t", dtype=str, encoding="latin-1")
pat = re.compile("|".join(LIG))
def lig(v):
    if not isinstance(v, str): return v
    return pat.sub(lambda m: m.group(0).replace("oe", "œ"), v)
c_ortho = [c for c in df.columns if c.endswith("ortho") and c.endswith("_ortho")][0]
c_lemme = [c for c in df.columns if c.endswith("_lemme")][0]
before = df[c_ortho].str.contains("|".join(LIG)).sum()
df[c_ortho] = df[c_ortho].map(lig)
df[c_lemme] = df[c_lemme].map(lig)
print("rows respelled with a ligature:", int(before), " e.g.", df.loc[df[c_ortho].str.contains("œ", na=False), c_ortho].head(8).tolist())
tmp = "/tmp/claude-0/-home-claude/d28672eb-31f4-533e-85fc-00a96d822022/scratchpad/v04_check/scripts/lex_ligature_utf8.tsv"
df.to_csv(tmp, sep="\t", index=False, encoding="utf-8")
lex_lig, _ = compute.load_lexicon(tmp)
print("(b) ligature-spelled UTF-8 lexicon, v0.4 loader:", stats(lex_lig))
print("    keys still containing a ligature after load:", sum(1 for k in lex_lig if "œ" in k or "æ" in k))
same = stats(lex)[:3] == stats(lex_lig)[:3]
print("    identical share_oov / n_content / mean to the oe-spelled lexicon:", same)

# (d) v0.3-style loader = same loader without the folding step
src = open(os.path.join(os.path.dirname(compute.__file__), "compute.py"), encoding="utf-8").read()
src03 = src.replace('''    for c in ["ortho", "lemme"]:  # same spelling as the texts (ligatures, apostrophes)
        df[c] = df[c].map(lambda v: normalise(v) if isinstance(v, str) else v)
''', "")
assert src03 != src
ns = {"__name__": "compute03"}
exec(compile(src03, "compute03", "exec"), ns)
lex03, _ = ns["load_lexicon"](tmp)
m, lem = ns["measure"](ns["clean"](TEXT)[1], lex03)
print("(d) loader without folding on the ligature lexicon: share_oov", round(m["share_oov"], 3), "n_content", m["n_content"])

# (c) tiny lexicons
def tiny(rows):
    t = tempfile.NamedTemporaryFile("w", suffix=".tsv", delete=False, encoding="utf-8")
    t.write("ortho\tlemme\tcgram\tfreqfilms2\tfreqlivres\tfreqlemfilms2\tfreqlemlivres\tnbsyll\n" + "\n".join(rows) + "\n")
    t.close()
    lx, _ = compute.load_lexicon(t.name); os.unlink(t.name); return lx
small = tiny(["cœur\tcœur\tNOM\t200\t250\t320\t320\t1", "œil\toeil\tNOM\t90\t80\t120\t120\t1", "Œuvre\tŒuvre\tNOM\t10\t10\t20\t20\t1",
              "œufs\tœuf\tNOM\t10\t10\t40\t40\t1", "bat\tbattre\tVER\t10\t10\t50\t50\t1", "mon\tmon\tADJ:pos\t500\t500\t900\t900\t1",
              "a\tavoir\tAUX\t900\t900\t900\t900\t1", "mal\tmal\tNOM\t100\t100\t100\t100\t1"])
for t in ["Mon cœur bat.", "Mon CŒUR bat.", "Œil pour œil.", "Les œufs", "Mon coeur bat."]:
    m, lem = compute.measure(compute.clean(t)[1] if len(t.split())>2 else [t], small)
    print(f"(c) tiny lexicon, text {t!r}: share_oov={m['share_oov']:.2f} lemmas={lem}")
print("    keys:", sorted(small))

"""R1: a hyphen standing alone between spaces mid-sentence is read as a dialogue dash (capitalised word after it = initial)."""
import sys
sys.path.insert(0, "/tmp/claude-0/-home-claude/d28672eb-31f4-533e-85fc-00a96d822022/scratchpad/v04_check/scripts")

def run(t):
    lines = compute.clean(t)[1]
    sents, rows = compute.analyse(lines, lex)
    names = [tok for tok, low, nm in rows if nm]
    m, lem = compute.measure(lines, lex)
    return names, lem
cases = [
 ("mid-sentence dash, verb imperative",   "Papa dit - Viens vite, il pleut ! - et nous courons vers la maison.", ["Papa"], ["dire","venir","vite","pleuvoir","courir","maison"]),

 ("compound with hyphen no spaces untouched", "La grand-mère de Léa fait un gâteau au chocolat.", ["Léa"], None),
 ("range with hyphen between digits",     "Les élèves de 8 - 9 ans lisent une histoire. Marie lit.", ["Marie"], None),
 ("hyphen at line start (dialogue)",      "Le loup arrive.\n- Bonjour, dit-il.\n- Bonjour Loup, répond Léa.", None, None),
 ("self-test case",                       "Le soir, Léa cria - Regarde la lune ! - Elle brille, répondit Tom.", ["Léa","Tom"], None),

===== FILE readers/v04_check/scripts/t6_battery.py | 3218 bytes | sha256 cdba3f12aca1a06552abde7ca55c4698197eaada14933daca5e1a5c9c5102998 =====
# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
import sys, re, json
sys.path.insert(0, "/tmp/claude-0/-home-claude/d28672eb-31f4-533e-85fc-00a96d822022/scratchpad/v04_check/scripts")
from common import load, ART5
art5 = sys.argv[1] if len(sys.argv) > 1 else ART5
compute, lex = load(art5)
from answers import A

LIST_RE = re.compile(r"^(- |\d+\. )")
def render(pieces):
    out, prev = [], None
    for kind, txt in pieces:
        if kind == "title" and txt == "x":
            continue
        if out and prev is not None and LIST_RE.match(txt) and LIST_RE.match(prev):
            out[-1] += "\n" + txt
        else:
            out.append(txt)
        prev = txt
    return "\n\n".join(out)

def canon(s):
    s = compute.normalise(s)
    s = re.sub(r"^(titre|title)\s*:\s*", "", re.sub(r"[#*_]+", " ", s).strip(), flags=re.I)
    return re.sub(r"[\W_]+", "", s.lower())

rows, problems = [], []
for a in A:
    text = render(a["pieces"])
    info = compute.clean_info(text)
    out_lines = [canon(l) for l in info["lines"]]
    out_title = canon(info["title"])
    exp_title = next((canon(t) for k, t in a["pieces"] if k == "title" and t != "x"), "")
    bodies = [(canon(t), t) for k, t in a["pieces"] if k == "body"]
    metas = [(canon(t), t) for k, t in a["pieces"] if k == "meta"]
    lost = [t for c, t in bodies if c not in out_lines and c != out_title]
    leaked = [t for c, t in metas if c in out_lines]
    title_ok = (out_title == exp_title)
    rec = {"ok": True, "finish_reason": "stop", "text": text}
    m, _ = compute.measure(info["lines"], lex)
    reason = compute.exclusion_reason(rec, m)
    fm = compute.flag_meta(text)
    exp_meta = len(metas)
    r = dict(id=a["id"], plaus=a["plaus"], genre=a["genre"], words=m["n_words"], n_meta=info["n_meta_lines"], exp_meta=exp_meta,
             title_ok=title_ok, lost=lost, leaked=leaked, excluded=reason, flag_meta=fm, got_title=info["title"], note=a["note"])
    rows.append(r)
    issue = (lost or leaked or not title_ok or reason or (fm and not metas))
    flagtxt = "FLAG" if fm else "    "
    print(f"{a['id']} {a['plaus'][:4]} {a['genre']:5s} words={m['n_words']:3d} meta {info['n_meta_lines']}/{exp_meta} title_ok={str(title_ok):5s} excl={reason or '-':8s} {flagtxt} {'<<< ISSUE' if issue else ''}")
    if lost: print("      genuine lines REMOVED:", [x[:70] for x in lost])
    if leaked: print("      meta lines LEFT IN TEXT:", [x[:70] for x in leaked])
    if not title_ok: print("      title found:", repr(info["title"]), "expected:", exp_title[:50])
    if reason: print("      EXCLUDED as", reason)
    if fm and not metas: print("      genuine text FLAGGED by flag_meta")
json.dump(rows, open("battery_results.json", "w"), ensure_ascii=False, indent=1)
pl = [r for r in rows if r["plaus"] == "plausible"]
ed = [r for r in rows if r["plaus"] == "edge"]
def summ(rs, label):
    print(f"\n{label}: n={len(rs)} | genuine line removed in {sum(bool(r['lost']) for r in rs)} | meta left in {sum(bool(r['leaked']) for r in rs)} | "
          f"title wrong in {sum(not r['title_ok'] for r in rs)} | excluded {sum(bool(r['excluded']) for r in rs)} | genuine flagged {sum(r['flag_meta'] and r['exp_meta']==0 for r in rs)}")
summ(pl, "PLAUSIBLE"); summ(ed, "EDGE")

import re
LIST_RE = re.compile(r"^(- |\d+\. )")
def render(pieces):
    out, prev = [], None
    for kind, txt in pieces:
        if kind == "title" and txt == "x":
            continue
        if out and prev is not None and LIST_RE.match(txt) and LIST_RE.match(prev):

# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
import sys

import re

def mean(lines):
    m, _ = compute.measure(lines, lex); return m["mean_zipf_content"], m["n_content"]
def truth_lines(a):
    return [compute.clean_info(p[1])["lines"][0] if False else p[1] for p in a["pieces"] if p[0] == "body"]
def canon_lines(a):
    # expected body lines, passed through the same plain-text step but with no preface/postface logic
    out = []
    for k, t in a["pieces"]:
        if k == "body":
            out.append(" ".join(re.sub(r"[#*_]+", " ", compute.normalise(t)).split()))
    return out

    a = byid[id_]
    got = compute.clean_info(render(a["pieces"]))["lines"]
    want = canon_lines(a)
    mg, ng = mean(got); mw, nw = mean(want)
    print(f"{id_}: mean Zipf got {mg:.4f} (n_content {ng}) vs truth {mw:.4f} (n_content {nw})  delta {mg-mw:+.4f}")

"""v0.4.3 check, W1 item 2: run compute.py (python compute.py LEXIQUE RAW_GLOB OUT_CSV) on raw files written by generate.py

===== FILE readers/v041_check/scripts/mock_weird2.py | 5649 bytes | sha256 c50b62760e8a13aac97c63bb752dd9f969095ac686e049d8a60db347ab4c1e79 =====
"""Offline stand-in for the provider APIs, v0.4.2 check. Answers the K-th request of a route with a malformed-but-HTTP-200 body (WEIRD shape),
every other request normally. Superset of mock_weird.py (same shapes) plus shapes for the v0.4.2 rules; logs what it sent. Not data.
env: WEIRD shape name, WEIRD_K (default 2): 1-based index of the request that gets the weird body, WEIRD_LOG: jsonl path."""
import json, os, sys, threading
from http.server import ThreadingHTTPServer, BaseHTTPRequestHandler

SHAPE = os.environ.get("WEIRD", "none"); K = int(os.environ.get("WEIRD_K", "2")); LOG = os.environ.get("WEIRD_LOG")
N = {}; LOCK = threading.Lock()
TEXT = "Le jardin des abeilles\n\n" + " ".join(["Le chat joue dans le jardin avec un petit chien."] * 6)


def normal(route, model):
    if route == "anthropic":
        return {"model": model, "content": [{"type": "text", "text": TEXT}], "stop_reason": "end_turn", "usage": {"input_tokens": 40, "output_tokens": 300}}
    return {"model": model, "choices": [{"message": {"content": TEXT}, "finish_reason": "stop"}], "usage": {"prompt_tokens": 40, "completion_tokens": 300}}


def set_usage(b, route, tin, tout):
    if route == "anthropic":
        b["usage"] = {"input_tokens": tin, "output_tokens": tout}
    else:
        b["usage"] = {"prompt_tokens": tin, "completion_tokens": tout}


def weird(route, model, shape):
    b = normal(route, model)
    if shape == "list": return json.dumps([1, 2, 3]).encode()
    if shape == "empty_choices":
        return json.dumps({"content": [], "usage": {"input_tokens": 40, "output_tokens": 0}} if route == "anthropic" else {"choices": [], "usage": {"prompt_tokens": 40, "completion_tokens": 0}}).encode()
    if shape == "usage_null": b["usage"] = None
    if shape == "tokens_null": set_usage(b, route, None, None)
    if shape == "content_number":
        if route == "anthropic": b["content"][0]["text"] = 5
        else: b["choices"][0]["message"]["content"] = 5
    if shape == "surrogate":
        return json.dumps(b).replace("Le jardin", "\\ud800 Le jardin", 1).encode()
    if shape == "non_utf8": return b'{"model": "x", "content": "\xff\xfe\xfa"}'
    if shape == "empty_body": return b""
    if shape == "content_parts_bad": b["choices"] = [{"message": {"content": [1, 2]}, "finish_reason": "stop"}] if route != "anthropic" else b["choices"] if "choices" in b else None
    # --- v0.4.2 shapes
    if shape == "usage_missing": del b["usage"]
    if shape == "usage_empty": b["usage"] = {}
    if shape == "out_missing": b["usage"] = {"input_tokens": 40} if route == "anthropic" else {"prompt_tokens": 40}
    if shape == "tokens_str": set_usage(b, route, "40", "300")
    if shape == "tokens_float": set_usage(b, route, 40.0, 300.0)
    if shape == "tokens_neg": set_usage(b, route, -5, -5)
    if shape == "tokens_bool": set_usage(b, route, True, True)
    if shape == "tokens_inf": set_usage(b, route, float("inf"), float("inf"))
    if shape == "finish_num":
        if route == "anthropic": b["stop_reason"] = 5
        else: b["choices"][0]["finish_reason"] = 5
    if shape == "model_num": b["model"] = 5
    if shape == "model_surrogate":
        b["model"] = "SURR"
        return json.dumps(b).replace("SURR", "\\ud800x", 1).encode()
    if shape == "finish_surrogate":  # v0.4.3 check: same construction as the main session's mock_weird.py
        if route == "anthropic": b["stop_reason"] = "FINISH_SURROGATE"
        else: b["choices"][0]["finish_reason"] = "FINISH_SURROGATE"
        return json.dumps(b).replace('"FINISH_SURROGATE"', '"\\ud800stop"').encode()
    if shape == "id_surrogate":  # v0.4.3 check: a lone surrogate in a field generate.py keeps only inside "response"
        b["id"] = "SURR"
        return json.dumps(b).replace("SURR", "\\ud800id", 1).encode()
    if shape == "text_null":
        if route == "anthropic": b["content"] = []
        else: b["choices"][0]["message"]["content"] = None
    if shape == "html_200": return b"<html><body>Bad gateway</body></html>"
    return json.dumps(b).encode()


class H(BaseHTTPRequestHandler):
    def log_message(self, *a): pass

    def do_POST(self):
        body = json.loads(self.rfile.read(int(self.headers["Content-Length"])).decode())
        route = self.path.strip("/"); model = body["model"]
        if route == "openai" and "temperature" in body:
            msg = b'{"error": {"message": "Unsupported parameter: temperature is not supported with this model."}}'
            self.send_response(400); self.send_header("Content-Length", str(len(msg))); self.end_headers(); self.wfile.write(msg); return
        with LOCK:
            N[route] = N.get(route, 0) + 1; k = N[route]
        is_weird = (SHAPE != "none" and (k == K or os.environ.get("WEIRD_ALL") == "1"))  # WEIRD_ALL=1: every request is weird (v0.4.3 check)
        data = weird(route, model, SHAPE) if is_weird else json.dumps(normal(route, model)).encode()
        if LOG:
            with LOCK:
                with open(LOG, "a", encoding="utf-8") as f:
                    f.write(json.dumps({"route": route, "k": k, "weird": is_weird, "shape": SHAPE if is_weird else None, "body": data.decode("utf-8", "replace")}) + "\n")
        self.send_response(200); self.send_header("Content-Type", "application/json"); self.send_header("Content-Length", str(len(data))); self.end_headers(); self.wfile.write(data)

    def do_GET(self):
        data = b'{"data": []}'; self.send_response(200); self.send_header("Content-Length", str(len(data))); self.end_headers(); self.wfile.write(data)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    ThreadingHTTPServer(("127.0.0.1", int(sys.argv[1])), H).serve_forever()

"""Offline stand-in for the provider APIs that answers the K-th request of a route with a malformed-but-HTTP-200 body (WEIRD shape),

"""
import json, os, sys, threading
from http.server import ThreadingHTTPServer, BaseHTTPRequestHandler
SHAPE = os.environ.get("WEIRD", "none"); K = int(os.environ.get("WEIRD_K", "2"))
N = {}; LOCK = threading.Lock()
TEXT = "Le jardin des abeilles\n\n" + " ".join(["Le chat joue dans le jardin avec un petit chien."] * 6)

def normal(route, model):
    if route == "anthropic":
        return {"model": model, "content": [{"type": "text", "text": TEXT}], "stop_reason": "end_turn", "usage": {"input_tokens": 40, "output_tokens": 300}}
    return {"model": model, "choices": [{"message": {"content": TEXT}, "finish_reason": "stop"}], "usage": {"prompt_tokens": 40, "completion_tokens": 300}}

def weird(route, model, shape):
    b = normal(route, model)
    if shape == "list": return json.dumps([1, 2, 3]).encode()
    if shape == "empty_choices":
        return json.dumps({"content": [], "usage": {"input_tokens": 40, "output_tokens": 0}} if route == "anthropic" else {"choices": [], "usage": {"prompt_tokens": 40, "completion_tokens": 0}}).encode()
    if shape == "usage_null": b["usage"] = None
    if shape == "usage_missing": b.pop("usage", None)
    if shape == "usage_empty": b["usage"] = {}
    if shape == "model_surrogate":
        b["model"] = "MODEL_SURROGATE"
        return json.dumps(b).replace('"MODEL_SURROGATE"', '"\\ud800x"').encode()

        if route == "anthropic": b["stop_reason"] = "FINISH_SURROGATE"
        else: b["choices"][0]["finish_reason"] = "FINISH_SURROGATE"
        return json.dumps(b).replace('"FINISH_SURROGATE"', '"\\ud800stop"').encode()
    if shape == "tokens_null": b["usage"] = {"input_tokens": None, "output_tokens": None} if route == "anthropic" else {"prompt_tokens": None, "completion_tokens": None}
    if shape == "content_number":
        if route == "anthropic": b["content"][0]["text"] = 5
        else: b["choices"][0]["message"]["content"] = 5
    if shape == "surrogate":
        s = json.dumps(b).replace("Le jardin", "\\ud800 Le jardin", 1)
        return s.encode()
    if shape == "non_utf8": return b'{"model": "x", "content": "\xff\xfe\xfa"}'
    if shape == "empty_body": return b""
    if shape == "content_parts_bad": b["choices"] = [{"message": {"content": [1, 2]}, "finish_reason": "stop"}] if route != "anthropic" else b["choices"] if "choices" in b else None
    return json.dumps(b).encode()

class H(BaseHTTPRequestHandler):
    def log_message(self, *a): pass
    def do_POST(self):
        body = json.loads(self.rfile.read(int(self.headers["Content-Length"])).decode())
        route = self.path.strip("/"); model = body["model"]
        if route == "openai" and "temperature" in body:
            msg = b'{"error": {"message": "Unsupported parameter: temperature is not supported with this model."}}'
            self.send_response(400); self.send_header("Content-Length", str(len(msg))); self.end_headers(); self.wfile.write(msg); return
        with LOCK:
            N[route] = N.get(route, 0) + 1; k = N[route]
        data = weird(route, model, SHAPE) if (k == K and SHAPE != "none") else json.dumps(normal(route, model)).encode()
        self.send_response(200); self.send_header("Content-Type", "application/json"); self.send_header("Content-Length", str(len(data))); self.end_headers(); self.wfile.write(data)
    def do_GET(self):
        data = b'{"data": []}'; self.send_response(200); self.send_header("Content-Length", str(len(data))); self.end_headers(); self.wfile.write(data)

if __name__ == "__main__":
    ThreadingHTTPServer(("127.0.0.1", int(sys.argv[1])), H).serve_forever()

                weird_rec = rec; break

                  f"unreadable_kept={'yes' if keeps else ('key, differs' if unread else 'no')} "

===== FILE readers/v041_check/scripts/n3_n5_cases_v042.py | 9901 bytes | sha256 a89174a971e9db556078f73360adeb87dfd2e72d5cc47f6b24bc1de3ac82a29d =====
# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
"""N3 (lines without letters) and N5 (Voici, Note, closing lines): cases written for this check, v0.4 against v0.4.1."""
import itertools
import re
import sys
sys.path.insert(0, "/tmp/claude-0/-home-claude/d28672eb-31f4-533e-85fc-00a96d822022/scratchpad/v041_check/scripts")
from lib41 import load, verdict

new, lex = load("v042")
old, _ = load("v04")
S1 = "Un petit lapin sortit de son terrier. Il regarda le ciel bleu et les nuages blancs."
S2 = "Puis il courut dans le pré pour rejoindre ses amis. Ils jouèrent jusqu'au soir."
S3 = "Quand la nuit tomba, le lapin rentra chez lui et s'endormit."


def canon(s):
    s = new.normalise(s)
    s = re.sub(r"^(titre|title)\s*:\s*", "", re.sub(r"[#*_]+", " ", s).strip(), flags=re.I)
    return re.sub(r"[\W_]+", "", s.lower())


# ------------------------------------------------------------------ N3: separators at every slot
SEPS = ["---", "***", "___", "* * *", "- - -", "—————", "=====", "~~~~", ". . .", "• • •", "—"]
PRE = "Voici une histoire pour des élèves de 8 à 9 ans :"
TITLE = "# Le lapin curieux"
BODY = [S1, S2, S3]
POST = ["Bonne lecture !", "N'hésitez pas à me demander une autre version."]


def build(slots):
    """slots: dict slot index -> separator. 0 before preface, 1 preface/title, 2 title/body, 3 between body 1 and 2,
    4 body/closings, 5 between closings, 6 after the last closing."""
    out = []
    def sep(i):
        if i in slots:
            out.append(slots[i])
    sep(0); out.append(PRE); sep(1); out.append(TITLE); sep(2); out.append(BODY[0]); sep(3); out += BODY[1:]
    sep(4); out.append(POST[0]); sep(5); out.append(POST[1]); sep(6)
    return "\n\n".join(out)


def check_n3(mod):
    bad, n = [], 0
    combos = [dict()] + [{i: s} for i in range(7) for s in SEPS] + \
             [{i: s1, j: s2} for i, j in itertools.combinations(range(7), 2) for s1 in SEPS[:5] for s2 in SEPS[:5]]
    for slots in combos:
        text = build(slots)
        info = mod.clean_info(text)
        got = [canon(l) for l in info["lines"]]
        ok = (canon(info["title"]) == canon(TITLE) and got == [canon(b) for b in BODY] and info["n_meta_lines"] == 3)
        n += 1
        if not ok:
            bad.append((slots, info["title"], info["n_meta_lines"], len(got)))
    return n, bad


for name, mod in (("v0.4", old), ("v0.4.1", new)):
    n, bad = check_n3(mod)
    print(f"N3 {name}: {n} layouts with 11 kinds of separator line at 7 positions (one or two per answer): {len(bad)} wrong"
          + (f"; first: {bad[0]}" if bad else ""))

# a separator line that carries a letter must stay a line (not dropped): "--- fin ---" is text
t = "Le lapin curieux\n\n" + S1 + "\n\n--- fin ---\n\n" + S2
print("N3 control, '--- fin ---' keeps its letters:", new.clean_info(t)["lines"])
# an answer made only of separators
print("N3 control, answer '---' only:", verdict(new, lex, "---")["excl"], "| '---\\n---':", verdict(new, lex, "---\n---")["excl"])
# digits-only line (page number, list marker alone) is also dropped; check it does not change the measure

print("N3 control, digit-only lines: words v0.4 %d v0.4.1 %d, mean %.4f / %.4f" % (a["words"], b["words"], a["mean"], b["mean"]))

# ------------------------------------------------------------------ N5: Voici
print("\nN5 'Voici' first lines (answer without title line, 2 body paragraphs); 'removed' = first paragraph dropped as preface")
REST = "\n\n" + S2 + " " + S3
VOICI = [
    ("genuine", "Voici Tom, un garçon de CM1 qui rêvait de voler. Chaque matin, il regardait les oiseaux dans la cour de l'école."),

    ("genuine", "Voici le village de Saint-Luc, au niveau de la rivière. Les maisons y sont bâties sur des pilotis."),
    ("genuine", "Voici Léa, une élève qui n'était pas adaptée au bruit de la cantine. Elle préférait la bibliothèque."),
    ("preface", "Voici une histoire sur les volcans."),

    ("preface", "Voici un petit texte sur les volcans."),

    good_new = (removed_new == (kind == "preface"))
    good_old = (removed_old == (kind == "preface"))
    cnt[kind][0] += good_old
    cnt[kind][1] += good_new
    print(f"  {kind:8s} v0.4 {'removed' if removed_old else 'kept   '} {'ok' if good_old else 'XX'} | v0.4.1 {'removed' if removed_new else 'kept   '} {'ok' if good_new else 'XX'} | {first[:70]}")

# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
"""N3 (lines without letters) and N5 (Voici, Note, closing lines): cases written for this check, v0.4 against v0.4.1."""
import itertools
import re
import sys
sys.path.insert(0, "/tmp/claude-0/-home-claude/d28672eb-31f4-533e-85fc-00a96d822022/scratchpad/v041_check/scripts")
from lib41 import load, verdict

new, lex = load("v043")
old, _ = load("v04")
S1 = "Un petit lapin sortit de son terrier. Il regarda le ciel bleu et les nuages blancs."
S2 = "Puis il courut dans le pré pour rejoindre ses amis. Ils jouèrent jusqu'au soir."
S3 = "Quand la nuit tomba, le lapin rentra chez lui et s'endormit."


def canon(s):
    s = new.normalise(s)
    s = re.sub(r"^(titre|title)\s*:\s*", "", re.sub(r"[#*_]+", " ", s).strip(), flags=re.I)
    return re.sub(r"[\W_]+", "", s.lower())


# ------------------------------------------------------------------ N3: separators at every slot
SEPS = ["---", "***", "___", "* * *", "- - -", "—————", "=====", "~~~~", ". . .", "• • •", "—"]
PRE = "Voici une histoire pour des élèves de 8 à 9 ans :"
TITLE = "# Le lapin curieux"
BODY = [S1, S2, S3]
POST = ["Bonne lecture !", "N'hésitez pas à me demander une autre version."]


def build(slots):
    """slots: dict slot index -> separator. 0 before preface, 1 preface/title, 2 title/body, 3 between body 1 and 2,
    4 body/closings, 5 between closings, 6 after the last closing."""
    out = []
    def sep(i):
        if i in slots:
            out.append(slots[i])
    sep(0); out.append(PRE); sep(1); out.append(TITLE); sep(2); out.append(BODY[0]); sep(3); out += BODY[1:]
    sep(4); out.append(POST[0]); sep(5); out.append(POST[1]); sep(6)
    return "\n\n".join(out)


def check_n3(mod):
    bad, n = [], 0
    combos = [dict()] + [{i: s} for i in range(7) for s in SEPS] + \
             [{i: s1, j: s2} for i, j in itertools.combinations(range(7), 2) for s1 in SEPS[:5] for s2 in SEPS[:5]]
    for slots in combos:
        text = build(slots)
        info = mod.clean_info(text)
        got = [canon(l) for l in info["lines"]]
        ok = (canon(info["title"]) == canon(TITLE) and got == [canon(b) for b in BODY] and info["n_meta_lines"] == 3)
        n += 1
        if not ok:
            bad.append((slots, info["title"], info["n_meta_lines"], len(got)))
    return n, bad


for name, mod in (("v0.4", old), ("v0.4.1", new)):
    n, bad = check_n3(mod)
    print(f"N3 {name}: {n} layouts with 11 kinds of separator line at 7 positions (one or two per answer): {len(bad)} wrong"
          + (f"; first: {bad[0]}" if bad else ""))

# a separator line that carries a letter must stay a line (not dropped): "--- fin ---" is text
t = "Le lapin curieux\n\n" + S1 + "\n\n--- fin ---\n\n" + S2
print("N3 control, '--- fin ---' keeps its letters:", new.clean_info(t)["lines"])
# an answer made only of separators
print("N3 control, answer '---' only:", verdict(new, lex, "---")["excl"], "| '---\\n---':", verdict(new, lex, "---\n---")["excl"])
# digits-only line (page number, list marker alone) is also dropped; check it does not change the measure
t_digits = "Le lapin curieux\n\n" + S1 + "\n\n1\n\n" + S2 + "\n\n2.\n\n" + S3
a, b = verdict(old, lex, t_digits), verdict(new, lex, t_digits)
print("N3 control, digit-only lines: words v0.4 %d v0.4.1 %d, mean %.4f / %.4f" % (a["words"], b["words"], a["mean"], b["mean"]))

# ------------------------------------------------------------------ N5: Voici
print("\nN5 'Voici' first lines (answer without title line, 2 body paragraphs); 'removed' = first paragraph dropped as preface")
REST = "\n\n" + S2 + " " + S3

    ("genuine", "Voici le village de Saint-Luc, au niveau de la rivière. Les maisons y sont bâties sur des pilotis."),

===== FILE readers/v041_check/scripts/scan_records43.py | 1721 bytes | sha256 9af50e9d763c1a0607b505eda86ecea3e116b5472db0384c477e5c60f9e6e406 =====
# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
"""v0.4.3 check: scan every raw record written by generate.py v0.4.3 in this session's run folders and compare the money counted on
each record (cost_usd + cost_usd_possibly_charged) with the reservation of its model (500 input tokens + 4000 output tokens at the
model's prices). Also counts unreadable raw lines and non-UTF-8 files. Reads only; usage: scan_records43.py [folder-glob ...]"""
import glob
import json
import sys

ROOT = "/tmp/claude-0/-home-claude/d28672eb-31f4-533e-85fc-00a96d822022/scratchpad/v041_check/runs"
PRICES = {"anthropic_balanced": (3.0, 15.0), "openai_balanced": (2.0, 10.0), "mistral_balanced": (0.5, 2.0)}
globs = sys.argv[1:] or ["*v043*"]
files = sorted({f for g in globs for f in glob.glob(f"{ROOT}/{g}/data/raw/*.jsonl")})
n = bad = nonutf = over = 0
worst = 0.0
dirs = set()
for f in files:
    dirs.add(f.split("/runs/")[1].split("/")[0])
    data = open(f, "rb").read()
    try:
        data.decode("utf-8")
    except UnicodeDecodeError:
        nonutf += 1
    for line in data.decode("utf-8", "replace").split("\n"):
        if not line:
            continue
        try:
            r = json.loads(line)
        except ValueError:
            bad += 1
            continue
        pin, pout = PRICES[r["model"]]
        w = (pin * 500 + pout * 4000) / 1e6
        c = float(r.get("cost_usd", 0)) + float(r.get("cost_usd_possibly_charged", 0))
        n += 1
        worst = max(worst, c / w)
        if c > w + 1e-9:
            over += 1
print(f"{len(dirs)} run folders, {len(files)} raw files, {n} records | unreadable lines {bad} | non-UTF-8 files {nonutf} | "
      f"records above their reservation {over} | highest counted/reservation {worst:.3f}")

===== FILE readers/v041_check/scripts/t_new10_v042.py | 3628 bytes | sha256 1ff92702a42fd6aa5d66a76582251c5d07872ce47e78659f77cb2af46c77c819 =====
# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
"""Run the 10 new answers (answers41.py) through the cleaning, refusal and flag rules of v0.4 and v0.4.1.
Same checks as the v0.4 battery (t6_battery.py): genuine line removed, meta line left in, wrong title, excluded, flagged."""
import json
import re
import sys
sys.path.insert(0, "/tmp/claude-0/-home-claude/d28672eb-31f4-533e-85fc-00a96d822022/scratchpad/v041_check/scripts")
from lib41 import load
from answers41 import A

LIST_RE = re.compile(r"^(- |\d+\. )")


def render(pieces):
    out, prev = [], None
    for kind, txt in pieces:
        if kind == "title" and txt == "x":
            continue
        if out and prev is not None and LIST_RE.match(txt) and LIST_RE.match(prev):
            out[-1] += "\n" + txt
        else:
            out.append(txt)
        prev = txt
    return "\n\n".join(out)


def run(which):
    mod, lex = load(which)

    def canon(s):
        s = mod.normalise(s)
        s = re.sub(r"^(titre|title)\s*:\s*", "", re.sub(r"[#*_]+", " ", s).strip(), flags=re.I)
        return re.sub(r"[\W_]+", "", s.lower())

    rows = []
    for a in A:
        text = render(a["pieces"])
        info = mod.clean_info(text)
        out_lines = [canon(l) for l in info["lines"]]
        out_title = canon(info["title"])
        exp_title = next((canon(t) for k, t in a["pieces"] if k == "title" and t != "x"), "")
        bodies = [(canon(t), t) for k, t in a["pieces"] if k == "body"]
        metas = [(canon(t), t) for k, t in a["pieces"] if k == "meta"]
        lost = [t for c, t in bodies if c not in out_lines and c != out_title]
        leaked = [t for c, t in metas if c in out_lines]
        m, _ = mod.measure(info["lines"], lex)
        rec = {"ok": True, "finish_reason": "stop", "text": text}
        reason = mod.exclusion_reason(rec, m)
        fm = mod.flag_meta(text)
        rows.append(dict(id=a["id"], plaus=a["plaus"], words=m["n_words"], n_meta=info["n_meta_lines"], exp_meta=len(metas),
                         title_ok=(out_title == exp_title), lost=lost, leaked=leaked, excl=reason, flag=fm,
                         mean=m["mean_zipf_content"], note=a["note"]))
    return rows


old, new = run("v041"), run("v042")
for r0, r1 in zip(old, new):
    def line(r):
        issue = bool(r["lost"] or r["leaked"] or not r["title_ok"] or r["excl"] or (r["flag"] and r["exp_meta"] == 0))
        return (f"words={r['words']:3d} meta {r['n_meta']}/{r['exp_meta']} title_ok={str(r['title_ok']):5s} "
                f"excl={r['excl'] or '-':6s} flag={'Y' if r['flag'] else '-'} mean={r['mean']:.3f} {'<<< ISSUE' if issue else ''}")
    print(f"{r1['id']} {r1['plaus'][:5]} | v0.4.1 {line(r0)}")
    print(f"{'':10s} | v0.4.2 {line(r1)}")
    for tag, r in (("v0.4.1", r0), ("v0.4.2", r1)):
        if r["lost"]:
            print(f"{'':10s}   {tag}: genuine line REMOVED:", [x[:60] for x in r["lost"]])
        if r["leaked"]:
            print(f"{'':10s}   {tag}: meta line LEFT IN:", [x[:60] for x in r["leaked"]])
        if r["flag"] and r["exp_meta"] == 0:
            print(f"{'':10s}   {tag}: genuine text FLAGGED")
json.dump({"v041": old, "v042": new}, open("new10_results_v042.json", "w"), ensure_ascii=False, indent=1)


def summ(rows, label):
    print(f"{label}: n={len(rows)} | genuine line removed in {sum(bool(r['lost']) for r in rows)} | meta left in {sum(bool(r['leaked']) for r in rows)} | "
          f"title wrong {sum(not r['title_ok'] for r in rows)} | excluded {sum(bool(r['excl']) for r in rows)} | "
          f"genuine flagged {sum(r['flag'] and r['exp_meta'] == 0 for r in rows)}")


print()
summ(old, "v0.4.1")

# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
"""Run the 10 new answers (answers41.py) through the cleaning, refusal and flag rules of v0.4 and v0.4.2.
Same checks as the v0.4 battery (t6_battery.py): genuine line removed, meta line left in, wrong title, excluded, flagged."""
import json
import re
import sys
sys.path.insert(0, "/tmp/claude-0/-home-claude/d28672eb-31f4-533e-85fc-00a96d822022/scratchpad/v042_check/scripts")
from lib41 import load
from answers41 import A

LIST_RE = re.compile(r"^(- |\d+\. )")


def render(pieces):
    out, prev = [], None
    for kind, txt in pieces:
        if kind == "title" and txt == "x":
            continue
        if out and prev is not None and LIST_RE.match(txt) and LIST_RE.match(prev):
            out[-1] += "\n" + txt
        else:
            out.append(txt)
        prev = txt
    return "\n\n".join(out)


def run(which):
    mod, lex = load(which)

    def canon(s):
        s = mod.normalise(s)
        s = re.sub(r"^(titre|title)\s*:\s*", "", re.sub(r"[#*_]+", " ", s).strip(), flags=re.I)
        return re.sub(r"[\W_]+", "", s.lower())

    rows = []
    for a in A:
        text = render(a["pieces"])
        info = mod.clean_info(text)
        out_lines = [canon(l) for l in info["lines"]]
        out_title = canon(info["title"])
        exp_title = next((canon(t) for k, t in a["pieces"] if k == "title" and t != "x"), "")
        bodies = [(canon(t), t) for k, t in a["pieces"] if k == "body"]
        metas = [(canon(t), t) for k, t in a["pieces"] if k == "meta"]
        lost = [t for c, t in bodies if c not in out_lines and c != out_title]
        leaked = [t for c, t in metas if c in out_lines]
        m, _ = mod.measure(info["lines"], lex)
        rec = {"ok": True, "finish_reason": "stop", "text": text}
        reason = mod.exclusion_reason(rec, m)
        fm = mod.flag_meta(text)
        rows.append(dict(id=a["id"], plaus=a["plaus"], words=m["n_words"], n_meta=info["n_meta_lines"], exp_meta=len(metas),
                         title_ok=(out_title == exp_title), lost=lost, leaked=leaked, excl=reason, flag=fm,
                         mean=m["mean_zipf_content"], note=a["note"]))
    return rows


old, new = run("v042"), run("v043")
for r0, r1 in zip(old, new):
    def line(r):
        issue = bool(r["lost"] or r["leaked"] or not r["title_ok"] or r["excl"] or (r["flag"] and r["exp_meta"] == 0))
        return (f"words={r['words']:3d} meta {r['n_meta']}/{r['exp_meta']} title_ok={str(r['title_ok']):5s} "
                f"excl={r['excl'] or '-':6s} flag={'Y' if r['flag'] else '-'} mean={r['mean']:.3f} {'<<< ISSUE' if issue else ''}")
    print(f"{r1['id']} {r1['plaus'][:5]} | v0.4.2 {line(r0)}")
    print(f"{'':10s} | v0.4.3 {line(r1)}")
    for tag, r in (("v0.4.2", r0), ("v0.4.3", r1)):
        if r["lost"]:
            print(f"{'':10s}   {tag}: genuine line REMOVED:", [x[:60] for x in r["lost"]])
        if r["leaked"]:
            print(f"{'':10s}   {tag}: meta line LEFT IN:", [x[:60] for x in r["leaked"]])
        if r["flag"] and r["exp_meta"] == 0:
            print(f"{'':10s}   {tag}: genuine text FLAGGED")
json.dump({"v042": old, "v043": new}, open("new10_results_v043.json", "w"), ensure_ascii=False, indent=1)


def summ(rows, label):
    print(f"{label}: n={len(rows)} | genuine line removed in {sum(bool(r['lost']) for r in rows)} | meta left in {sum(bool(r['leaked']) for r in rows)} | "
          f"title wrong {sum(not r['title_ok'] for r in rows)} | excluded {sum(bool(r['excl']) for r in rows)} | "
          f"genuine flagged {sum(r['flag'] and r['exp_meta'] == 0 for r in rows)}")


print()
summ(old, "v0.4.2")
summ(new, "v0.4.3")

"""v0.4.3 check, W2 (word boundary after the new alternatives of REFUSAL_RE). Columns: v0.4.1, v0.4.2, v0.4.3.

from collections import Counter

def run_head(fname, cut_marker):
    src = open(os.path.join(HERE, fname), encoding="utf-8").read().split(cut_marker)[0]
    ns = {"__file__": os.path.join(HERE, fname)}
    with contextlib.redirect_stdout(io.StringIO()):
        exec(compile(src, fname, "exec"), ns)
    return ns


n24 = run_head("n2_n4_cases.py", "rows = []")

    r = verdict(mod, lex, text)

        return "EXCL"
    if r["excl"]:
        return r["excl"][:6]
    return "kept+FLAG" if r["flag"] else "kept"

    return out

    return sum(r[1][i] == what for r in res)

# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
"""v0.4.2 check, W4: for the 12 unlisted-opener clarifications of n2_recount.py, which sub-rule of flag_meta fires (v0.4.2 code):
META_RE on the edge lines, or the label-question rule (LABEL_Q_RE inside one sentence that contains '?'). Reads compute.py, edits nothing."""
import contextlib
import io
import os
import sys

HERE = os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))
sys.path.insert(0, HERE)
from lib41 import load

m42, lex = load("v042")
src = open(os.path.join(HERE, "n2_recount.py"), encoding="utf-8").read().split("def st(mod, t):")[0]
ns = {"__file__": os.path.join(HERE, "n2_recount.py")}
with contextlib.redirect_stdout(io.StringIO()):
    exec(compile(src, "n2_recount_head.py", "exec"), ns)
unl = ns["unl"]


def parts(mod, text):
    items = mod.edge_lines(text)
    edge = items if len(items) <= 5 else items[:2] + items[-3:]
    meta = label = False
    for line, _ in edge:
        if line.startswith(mod.DIALOGUE_START):
            continue
        unq = mod.QUOTED_RE.sub(" ", line)
        if mod.META_RE.search(unq):
            meta = True
        if any("?" in s and mod.LABEL_Q_RE.search(s) for s in mod.SENTENCE_END_RE.split(unq)):
            label = True
    return meta, label


tally = {"META only": 0, "label only": 0, "both": 0, "neither": 0}
for t in unl:
    meta, label = parts(m42, t)
    k = "both" if meta and label else "META only" if meta else "label only" if label else "neither"
    tally[k] += 1
    print(f"{k:10s} | {t[:90]}")
print("v0.4.2, 12 unlisted-opener clarifications:", tally)
assert m42.flag_meta("x") in (True, False)

@@ -2,7 +2,7 @@
 
 Audit of large language models asked to write French reading texts for primary pupils in French-speaking Switzerland. The grade is named in several ways: the bare HarmoS label ("de 5H"), the P label used in Vaud and Geneva ("de 5P"), the label with the region or with the age, the French grade ("de CE2"), the age only ("de 8 à 9 ans"), no level at all, and the same digit with the French collège suffix ("de 5e"). The question is whether a bare Swiss label gives texts of the same lexical demand as the matching age, and whether those texts follow the grade as the age-only texts do. No human participants.
 
-Status: design v0.4.1, before the pilot and before preregistration. Nothing here is a result.
+Status: design v0.4.2, before the pilot and before preregistration. Nothing here is a result.
